# ViewTouch Modern — development notes

A running, time-stamped record of changes made to this repository by each contributor
(people, Bob (Claude), Neil (Gemini)). It complements `CHANGELOG.md`, which is generated automatically from
commits by `tools/changelog.py` — don't hand-edit that one.

**How to add an entry**
- Add new entries at the **top** of the log (newest first).
- Heading: `### YYYY-MM-DD HH:MM ET — <who> — <short title>` (Eastern time, the store's clock).
- Say **what** changed, **why**, the **files**, and the **commit** (short hash) once pushed.
- Mention anything the next person must know (breaking changes, steps to run on the Pi).
- Pull before editing and commit this file together with the change it describes.
- Quickest: stage your change, then run
  `python3 modern/tools/log_dev_note.py --agent <who> --title "..." --what "..." --why "..." [--next "..."]`.
- **Enforced:** `.githooks/pre-commit` blocks any commit that changes files without a new, complete entry at
  the top of this Log. Turn it on once per clone with `bash modern/tools/setup_dev_hooks.sh`.
  Rules for everyone (people, Bob (Claude), Neil (Gemini)) are in `AGENTS.md` (also `CLAUDE.md`, `GEMINI.md`).

---

## 1. Shared Engineering & Logging Standard (`Neil` & `Bob`)

* **Canonical File**: `modern/vt-modern-development-notes.md` (never hand-edit `CHANGELOG.md`, which is generated by `tools/changelog.py`).
* **Agent Names**: **Neil (Gemini)** and **Bob (Claude)**. Keep literal file/product names (`CLAUDE.md`, `GEMINI.md`, `claude_label_templates.json`) unchanged.
* **Entry Format**: Prepend every change newest-first at the top of `## Log` using `### YYYY-MM-DD HH:MM ET — <Agent> — <short title>` with bullets **What**, **Why**, **Files** (required; the hook checks them), plus **Commit** and **Next person must know** when there is something to say.
* **Git Hook & Discipline**: Always `git pull` before editing. `.githooks/pre-commit` and `modern/tools/log_dev_note.py` block commits that touch repo files unless `modern/vt-modern-development-notes.md` is staged with a valid entry. Do not auto-generate empty skeleton entries or rewrite commit hashes post-commit.

## 2. Technical Handoff & Invariants ("Next Person Must Know")

* **Canonical Repo Path**: Always build and run from `/home/pi/viewtouch-main/modern` (never the legacy `~/Desktop/viewtouch-main` directory). Pi commands for the owner start with `cd ~/viewtouch-main` and end with `; beep3`.
* **viewtouch.db belongs to ViewTouch**: Live DB `~/.local/share/ViewTouch/ViewTouch/viewtouch.db`. ViewTouch keeps the store in memory and writes it back, so no other program may write to the DB while it runs (changes are lost or overwritten). The label utility opens it read-only (`mode=ro`). Any bulk import (menu, gift cards) must stop ViewTouch, back up the DB, then restart.
* **Branches**: `main` (what the Pi runs), `claude-label-utility` (Bob's working branch; `modern/tools/update_label_utility.sh` on the Pi merges it into `main` and pushes), and upload-only branches written by Pi scripts: `pi-shots` (label utility screenshots), `pi-vt-shots` (ViewTouch screenshots, `capture_vt_screenshots.sh`), `pi-logs` (`push_label_log.sh`). Bob's cloud workspace can't build or run Qt, so anything visual is captured on the Pi and fetched from these branches.
* **QML module loading**: The static `ViewTouch` QML module is built without the QTP0001 policy, so it lives under `qrc:/ViewTouch`, not `qrc:/qt/qml/ViewTouch`; `loadFromModule("ViewTouch", ...)` can't find it. Load screens by file as `main.cpp` (`vtmLoadModuleQml`) and the screenshot test (`loadViewTouchQml` in `tests/test_pos_flow.cpp`) do. `tests/test_pages_ui.cpp` and `tests/test_editor_ui.cpp` still use `loadFromModule` and fail on the Pi.
* **Compilation & SQLite WAL Safety**: The Pi 5 has 4 cores and 8 GB: compile with `-j4` (vt-server does; `VT_JOBS=2 vt-server` to use fewer) with the 2 GB swap file and ccache set up on 2026-11-03. Older advice was `-j1`/`-j2` (OOM with 200 MB swap). Never kill `vtmodern` while SQLite is writing in WAL mode (`viewtouch.db-wal`) without cleaning up `.lock` and `.running` files in `~/.local/share/ViewTouch/ViewTouch/`.
* **Zero-Header-Breakage Persistence**: Custom item attributes (`__retail__`, `__cost__`, `__parent__`, and upcoming `__pour_oz__` / `__abv__`) are stored inside `m.recipe` metadata rather than modifying core C++ struct headers, preventing full-tree recompiles and preserving backward/forward database compatibility.
* **Known Fragile Spots**:
  * `modern/android/AndroidManifest.xml`: `<activity-alias android:name="org.viewtouch.pos.Home">` must retain both `<meta-data android:name="android.app.lib_name".../>` lines or Lenovo tablets crash with `UnsatisfiedLinkError` on cold boot.
  * `modern/tools/install_label_utility.sh`: Never delete `/home/pi/Desktop/*label*.desktop` by wildcard, as it deletes the operator's `Update Label Utility` desktop icon.
  * Retail screen "Gift Cards" button: the page is `gift-card` (singular). Fixed 2026-11-02 in both `modern/seed/pages/retail-entry.json` and `modern/ui/qml/WidgetRetailEntry.qml`, which had `gift-cards`.
  * `vt-directv`: Page jump actions require both `"page"` and `"target"` keys.

## 3. Live Environment & Network Topology

* **Main POS Server**: Raspberry Pi 5 (`192.168.1.219`, MAC `d8:3a:dd:e5:47:a5`, POS TCP port `7719`, migrating to `192.168.6.x` on UniFi Dream Machine) running Debian 13 Trixie (Qt 6.8.2). Live DB: `~/.local/share/ViewTouch/ViewTouch/viewtouch.db`.
* **Lenovo Android Tablet Fleet**: Bar 1 (`192.168.1.220`), Bar 2 (`192.168.1.215`), and Bar 3 (`192.168.1.210`) managed via wireless ADB port `5555` (`vt-adb-ensure`, `vt-status`, `vt-server`, `vt-tablets`). Their addresses are fixed in the UniFi backend.
* **Printers**: Epson TM-T88 receipt/kitchen thermal printer at `192.168.1.77:9100` (ESC/POS); ZPL label printers "Shelf Labels" at `192.168.1.24:9100` (2x1 vinyl price labels; CUPS queue `BHDAN_PRICE_LABEL`) and "Stick Labels" at `192.168.1.25:9100` (cigar stick labels, different stock).
* **TVs (HCA, 2026-11-21)**: Sceptre TVs ignore the DIRECTV box's CEC standby; set each TV's no-signal power-off (sleep when no signal) so BOX off turns the TV off too (works at HCA, 2026-11-21). If a box says ON (`vt-directv check`) but shows nothing, unplug it and plug it back in. Channels by number: the Tune pages' **CH # (number pad)** key (command `directvKeypad`).
* **This repository is public** (`paigeent1/viewtouch`): never put passwords, PINs, API keys or customer data in the repo or these notes.

## 4. Codified Summary of Completed `vt-modern` Customizations

* **Retail POS, Inventory Workstation & Barcode Engine**:
  * Dedicated Retail POS Entry Mode (`ui/qml/WidgetRetailEntry.qml`, `seed/pages/retail-entry.json`) with 11x6 tactile matrix, PLU keypad, BOX <-> STICK toggle, 21+ age check modal, 5-level merchandise tree browser, 1-tap Retail <-> Hospitality switching, and per-terminal default mode (`startPage` / `vt-set-mode`).
  * Full-Screen Retail Item Workstation (`ui/qml/WidgetRetailItemEditor.qml`, `WidgetMenuBuilder.qml`) with 3-way mode chooser (Hospitality, Retail, Events), category mode toggles (`F&B`, `Retail`, `Event`), Alpha vs. UPC sort, `isRetail` flag (`{"__retail__", 1.0}`), 5-level hierarchy, parent/child unit cost cascade (`__cost__`, `__parent__`), and live Profit/Margin/Markup math.
  * Primary UPC (`m.barcodes[0]`) & Alt UPC (`m.barcodes[1..N]`) mapping across `app/pos_admin_*.cpp`, `pos_menu*.cpp`, and `WidgetMenuBuilder.qml`; GS1 Modulo-10 check-digit verification (`PosService::barcodeProblem()` in `app/pos_service.cpp`) with 11-to-12-digit auto-completion (`layoutcontroller.cpp`); and Unrecognized Barcode overlay (`u"unrecognized"_s` in `app/pos_loyalty.cpp` -> `ScanAddOverlay.qml`).
* **ViewTouch Label Utility v1.0 & Documentation**:
  * Unified `modern/tools/vt_label_utility.py` (`__version__ = "1.0"`, tag `label-utility-v1.0`), 100% read-only SQLite access, 203 DPI 1-bit full-label bitmap rendering (`^GFA`), integer-dot UPC-A, EAN-13, UPC-E, Code 39 and Code 128 barcodes, in-memory Primary/Alt/Carton UPC selection, `VTRoundedButton` dark UI, `Delete Template` & `Restore Standard Labels` buttons (added after the `label-utility-v1.0` tag, which is at `9f1c09f`), and `install_label_utility.sh` / `update_label_utility.sh`.
  * 24-page operator manual (`modern/docs/label-utility/ViewTouch_Label_Utility_User_Manual.pdf`, `build_manual.py` with `stack_toolbar`) and automated screenshot harvester (`capture_manual_screenshots.sh` -> `myfork/pi-shots`).
* **Printed Receipt & Touchscreen Choice Formatting**:
  * Always print free modifier choices (`print/tickets.cpp`), ESC/POS Font B (`9x17` dots, `\x1b\x4d\x01`, `4/3` width scaling) for 2-space indented sub-lines and `Opened <time> - closed <time>` timestamps (`print/document.cpp`), and compact `w.unit * 0.65` modifier text in `ui/qml/WidgetOrderList.qml`.
* **Customer Tracking, Gift Cards & Reporting**:
  * Split First/Last Name customer fields, cumulative spend/visit tracking, 1-tap closed-check customer assignment, and migration of 5,538 legacy customer profiles from `ilsa.mdb`.
  * 4-argument `PosService::sellGiftCard(number, amountCents, purchasedBy, issuedTo)` (`app/pos_customers.cpp`, `pos_service.hh`; the names are applied only to an existing card, and the Gift Card screen doesn't pass them, so PIN/For/By come from the PatronPal import; there is no bulk gift card import tool), `giftCardCashBelowCents` threshold, 8-column landscape gift card reports (Activated, Active Balances, Inactive, Transactions) (`core/report.cpp`, `ui/cpp/reportexport.cpp`, `ui/qml/WidgetReportView.qml`), 15-page manual with Pi screenshots (`modern/docs/gift-cards/ViewTouch_Gift_Cards_User_Manual.pdf`, `build_manual.py`), and offscreen screenshot runner (`modern/tools/capture_vt_screenshots.sh` -> `myfork/pi-vt-shots`).
* **Hospitality, Recipes, Check Splitting & DirecTV**:
  * Bill of Materials (`ingredients.json`) recipe costing/depletion, integer-cents check splitting with remainder penny allocation, and customized DirecTV seed pages (`seed/pages/directv-tune-*.json`).
* **Top Status Ribbon, UI Fit Fixes & Android Tablet Kiosk Fleet**:
  * Top Status Ribbon (`topRibbonEnabled`, time, battery, Wi-Fi, customer count, and live ZIP weather via `storeZip` / `fetchWeather` across `core/settings.hh`, `app/pos_session.hh`, `app/pos_service.hh`, `net/remote_session.hh`, `app/pos_admin_fields.cpp`, and `ui/qml/Main.qml`).
  * 64-bit (`arm64-v8a`) APK pipeline (`.github/workflows/modern-android.yml`, `build_apk.sh`, `cmake/BuildInfo.cmake`), Kiosk home alias (`AndroidManifest.xml`, `Kiosk.kt`), `ensureWirelessAdb`, PIN-protected reboot (`rebootWithPin`), yellow Terminal Name badge, JNI camera scanner (`BarcodeScanner.kt` -> `scanCamera`), and upstream fixes (`2e37ab3a` wake-from-sleep connection, `96b336f8` first-staff-login freeze fix).

Not started unless a Log entry says otherwise; files named here (`CustomerReceiptView.qml`, `stage_batch.py`, `corp_sync.py`, `store_config.json`) don't exist yet.

* **Alcohol Over-Service & Policy Audit Engine**: Per-item Pour Size (`__pour_oz__`) & ABV % (`__abv__`), Manager -> Settings policy thresholds, seat-to-customer mapping, pulsing Yellow / 2 Hz flashing Red banners in `WidgetOrderList.qml`, and `alcohol_policy_audit.jsonl` / PDF Audit Report generator.
* **Global Payments / EMV & Walkout Protection**: 1-tap Credit/Debit sales (`WidgetPaymentPanel.qml`), Bar Tab Pre-Auth & Auto-Incremental Holds (`AUTH` / `TRN_...`), Auto-Create Customer Profile from EMV Chip (`CARDHOLDER/NAME`), and Card-on-File Tokenization (`PMT_...`).
* **Front-End Ticket Counter, Mode Buttons & Importers**: Live item/quantity counter (`Items: X (Qty: Y)`) in `WidgetOrderList.qml`, Cashier/Manager mode buttons, variable-weight scale barcode parsing, separate `UPC`/`ALTUPC` CSV columns (`app/menu_import.cpp`), and ILSA batch staging (`stage_batch.py`).
* **Secondary HDMI Customer-Facing Display (CFD)**: Split-screen mode (`--customer-display window`) on Pi 5 Micro-HDMI 1 with 50% live receipt (`CustomerReceiptView.qml`) and 50% dynamic promo/pairing panel (`goesWith`).
  *Already working (not this item):* the basic customer display (`ui/qml/CustomerDisplay.qml`, `CustomerDisplayWindow.qml`: order, total, tips, thank-you, price checks), turned on in Manager -> Terminals -> Customer display (runs `vt-guest-display on`: HDMI-2 beside HDMI-1, not mirrored). Don't launch vtmodern with `--customer-display` while the monitors are mirrored.
* **Tablet customer displays with video (future idea)**: The Bar 1/2/3 tablets have no customer display today. If they get one later: add `-m qtmultimedia` to the Android workflow's `aqt install-qt` so `VideoPanel.qml` builds into the APK, and serve the Store Settings > Customer display video (an MP4 on the Pi) to the tablets over the network (a file on the Pi isn't reachable from a tablet). Until then tablets fall back to the picture/GIF.
* **Guest-screen raffles & contact entry (owner idea, 2026-11-03)**: With a second Wimaxit as a touch guest screen: guests type phone and email (needs an on-screen keyboard on the guest page, not just the phone keypad), and enter raffles; each entry prints legibly on a raffle-ticket printer (name, phone/email, drawing, number), and the entries are kept for the drawing.
* **Date of birth on receipts (owner decision 2026-11-06; not started)**: Print the buyer's date of birth on the receipt when an ID scan checked the age, as ILSA does. Today ViewTouch keeps the age passed, how, cashier, time, expired flag and the last 4 of the ID number on the check: it must also keep the DOB from the scan, print it on the receipt and on reprints (Receipt Lookup); legacy ILSA receipts carry theirs (imported with the history). Never in the repo, never in reports or exports; tests use made-up dates.
* **Customer accounts: key fob number, cell phone, ID link (owner 2026-11-05; not started)**: One customer record, found three ways: (1) a unique *customer number* printed as a barcode on a key fob or card (scan it and the customer is on the sale); (2) the *cell phone number*, typed by the customer on the guest screen or by the cashier; (3) *the customer's license*: an ID scan tied to the customer. Open questions for the owner: pre-numbered fobs or ViewTouch-issued numbers (gift cards start with 6, subscription cards with 9: fobs their own leading digit, or the subscription card doubles as the fob?); what sign-up requires (phone only, or name too); several fobs per customer (lost fob, spouse).
* **Price book name cleanup: "6+" means 6⅛ (owner 2026-11-05; not started; TOP of the list)**: *Changed 2026-11-06 (owner approved): names are fixed in ViewTouch, not ILSA (longer names; ⅛ ⅜ ⅝ ⅞ stored as is), on a "Price book cleanup" screen: lists names with 6+, 6++, a stray +, written-out fractions; suggested fix beside each (6+ X 52 -> 6⅛ X 52); approve all / edit one / skip; then fills Length / Ring gauge / Ring gauge 2 from the cleaned names with a preview. New items arriving from ILSA before cutover get the same suggestions.* The original idea below (an import rule) is kept only for those new items. In ILSA the owner typed "+" for an eighth (e.g. "6+" = 6 1/8; ILSA's Windows character set has no ⅛). Build it as a rule in vt-ilsa-items (applied on every import, so the cutover re-import keeps it), not a one-time edit: a number followed by "+" (regex (\d)\+(?!\d)) becomes an eighth, with a list of exceptions the owner marks (e.g. "2+1" deals, "+ LIGHTER", "6+ PK" if any). First a read-only review list (~/ilsa-items/plus-review.csv: UPC, name now, would become; Bob gave the owner the command). Open question: "6⅛" (compact; screens and labels show ⅛, receipts print 6 1/8; printable() maps ⅛ → 1/8 since 2026-11-05) or "6 1/8" (plain, longer on 2x1 labels). Same pass: ¢ ½ ¼ ¾ already come through since the Windows-1252 fix. *Also in the same pass (owner: "automation is key")*: fill the new cigar size fields (length, ringGauge, ringGauge2; added 2026-11-05) from names that spell the size, e.g. "6+ X 52", "6 1/8 X 52", "5X50", "6+X52/60" (length: whole + optional fraction, ½ ¼ ¾ or + = 1/8; then X; then the gauge, optional /second gauge for diademas); only fills empty fields, never overwrites a size typed in ViewTouch; same review list first (UPC, name, length, gauge, gauge 2) so odd matches (e.g. "2X50 PK" counts, "5X" pack sizes) can be marked as exceptions.
* **Test suite health (found 2026-11-03)**: GitHub "Modern checks" hasn't passed in 100+ runs. Fixed tonight: screens now load in tests (qml_load.hh) and a crash cut the run short. Still failing on main: the Menu Builder screen tests can't find item buttons (builderItem-<id>) in its grid (about 15 tests), and the gift-card page logs a Qt Quick Layouts "recursive rearrange" (WidgetGiftCard.qml; wrapped Labels already given Layout.preferredWidth). Plus a few unrelated (versions, forecast, self-order slip).
* **Retail receiving, inventory counts & movement (owner decisions 2026-11-04; not started)**: Goal: inventory counts right and movement data to plan purchases. No manager PIN anywhere in this (cashier or manager may be on duty; who did what is logged). No purchase orders (back orders make them confusing): receive what is on the invoice and in the box; no short / back-order list. Build in this order:
  1. *Receiving by scan*: pick the vendor, scan items (repeat scan +1, or type a count; case/carton UPCs add their pack size); quantities always go to on-hand. An item not on file: link the code to an existing item (saved as an alt UPC), quick-add (UPC filled in; name, department, cost, price, pack size), or set aside and finish at the end. Free goods (e.g. B7G1) are received as quantity. *Swap / adjustment* receiving for damaged cigars traded with reps (usually not the same product): + and - lines, no cost, a reason; counts net out. After saving, offer shelf labels for new items.
  2. *Movement screen* by item, brand, vendor: on hand, sold last 4/13/52 weeks, per week, weeks of supply, last received, suggested order (target weeks of supply minus on hand; a guide, not a PO), dead stock (no sales in 60/90 days), same weeks last year once there's history (or ILSA's, if imported). Physical counts (Stock Count) record differences as shrink.
  3. *System cost from the owner's worksheet*: he keeps what he wants system cost to be in a worksheet; import it (CSV/xlsx, preview, apply). Receiving NEVER changes system cost or the price book. OTP is already in his costs: no OTP handling at all.
  4. *Cost Differences review* (on screen, actionable): each line keeps invoice unit cost when the ticket shows it; difference vs system cost per unit and total; by vendor / brand (manufacturer > brand > product group) / item, with a summary that shows a brand-wide increase (e.g. 14 of 14 items up $0.18); lines can be ticked Reviewed; each vendor's last price per item kept for reference (same product from two vendors: system cost stays the one he chose). From a brand or vendor: *Adjust prices* (new cost: invoice / +$ / +% / unchanged; new retail: keep markup, +$, +%, hit target margin, or typed; rounding to his price endings; preview, untick, apply; offer labels).
  New fields: retail item system cost and *target margin %* (per item, default by department/brand; reports flag items under target). Vendor list exists (Manager > Inventory > Vendors: name, phone, account #, note): add rep name/cell, email, ordering method, active. Bring ILSA's vendor list and any target margins over with the ILSA data (owner is getting the ILSA database from Dropbox). Open questions for the owner: markup % vs same $ pass-through on cost increases; price endings by department; hide tiny cost differences or show all.
* **ILSA history import & beta tie-out (owner decisions 2026-11-04; importer and reports built 2026-11-07, branch bob/ilsa-history)**: *Built 2026-11-07:* `scripts/bin/vt-ilsa-history EXPORT [--dry-run] [--from/--to YYYY-MM-DD] [--replace]` imports the receipts, lines, tenders and ILSA's Z numbers into their own file, `ilsa-history.db` next to viewtouch.db (not viewtouch.db: it stays small, its backups and the standby copy don't carry 850k old lines; the history file can be rebuilt from the export any time). One transaction per business day (an ILSA Z, dated by its first receipt); re-runs skip days already in (an export's newest day is kept as partial and replaced by a newer export); real runs stop ViewTouch, back up the file, import, start ViewTouch again. Self-check per Z against Z_Taxes, Z_Tender (AMT_TEND; cash less all change), RegReconcile paid outs and the Z_Totals amounts (their meaning learned from the days), list in ~/ilsa-history/variances-<when>.csv and on the ILSA Differences report. Tenders are imported exactly as ILSA recorded them, never fixed (owner 2026-11-07: the 9/24 $90.66 card-batch gap is a sale rung as cash that was paid on the standalone card machine). Reports > ILSA History (new page): Daily Z (an ILSA day, ViewTouch's own checks that day too), Departments (when sold by default, owner 2026-11-07; "as ILSA: item's current department" to match the printouts), Item Movement (item / UPC / brand / department), GP by month / year, ILSA vs ViewTouch (ILSA's Z, the imported receipts, ViewTouch, with variances), ILSA Differences; Print, CSV, PDF. GP shown twice: as ILSA (lines without a cost at cost = retail, Adjusted Cost = Cost + Buydowns) and without no-cost lines (the true margin). Look Up Checks > ILSA history: receipts by day / # / UPC / total / name, read-only, marked ILSA; reprint as ILSA printed it (with the DOB, Store Settings' manager rule applies) or without. *First real import (owner, 2026-11-07, main 262ee15): ILSA 618 days, kp 1,822, copy bhdan 4,445; 6,885 days in all. `vt-ilsa-history --recheck` (branch bob/ilsa-recheck) redoes the self-check from the history file: receipts checked against lines + tax less their receipt discount; tenders only on Z's where ILSA saved tenders; the 2023-02-02 Z 5828 cash is labelled as the known mistyped amount; it prints what explains each kind of difference and the days whose dates look wrong (the history reported a 2004 first day: to be explained from its output).* *Dry run on the Pi 2026-11-07 22:35 (fixed on bob/ilsa-recheck2):* birth dates are in RegSale.BDAY_ENTERED; the fill now ties each row to its own receipt (copy, REG_NUM, RECEIPT_NUM), keeps only buyers' dates (1900 to 14 years before the sale: Access's empty 1899-12-30 is not one) and reports values / not imported from this copy / not a birth date / conflicting; the 'values' the finder printed were from the first 200,000 rows only, and the ILSA copy's low match is its rows after 2008-11-05, whose days came from the kp copy. Z's with no receipts (Z 1 of 2004-06-03, Z 3 of 2006-12-11) are not days of history. A Z ties when its tax is within 5 cents and its cash difference is explained (change left in card/gift/check tenders, cash given back, paid outs, cash on cancelled sales); receipts that don't add up on their own count only on Z's that don't tie; --explain-receipts EXPORT finds which column holds the receipts' extra $0.10 / $0.20 / $1.00. BHNM (Gaylordsville) comes as three copies with days in common: --plan / --import-plan pick each day from the copy that has it whole. *Stores (2026-11-07):* each ILSA copy belongs to a store (legacy_sources.store: BHDAN, BHNM next with `--prefix BHNM`); days are kept per store (one store's copy never replaces or skips another's); an export whose Sys_Pram names another store is refused unless --force; ILSA History, its Daily Z, Look Up Checks > ILSA history and the self-check (--recheck --store) choose a store or All, starting with Store Settings' 'ILSA history store' (default BHDAN). *Cashiers (2026-11-07, branch bob/ilsa-cashiers):* the importer keeps each copy's cashier numbers and names (no PINs or pay); Reports > ILSA History > Cashiers ties an ILSA cashier to a ViewTouch employee (or leaves them a former employee; the same number and name in the other copies follow); ILSA History > By Employee (sales by employee and item, with ViewTouch's own sales beside them) and the Reports screen's By Employee '+ ILSA history' switch use those ties. *Still open:* ILSA's Department Sales "Discount" column (receipt-level percent / subtotal discounts) isn't found yet: the importer keeps any DISC...AMT column of RegSale / PDetail and prints which it found; ViewTouch days have no cost per line or department at sale yet (comes with the CheckLine cost change). Before: The owner's ILSA database (Access .mdb) goes back to 2009; he downloads it from Dropbox to the Pi and runs `vt-ilsa-export` (branch bob/ilsa-export): every table to CSV in ~/ilsa-export (stays on the Pi), plus a summary of table names / row counts / columns to paste to Bob. Then: (1) *receipts* imported as closed checks marked ILSA, each line keeping its own name, UPC, dept/brand and price as sold; shown in Look Up Checks (reprint, gift receipt); never in today's drawers, X/Z or end-of-day; (2) *ILSA item reference*: all ~33,000 items read-only (UPCs + alt UPCs, name, mfg/brand/product group, last cost/price, first/last sold); the owner plans to trim the active list to under 20,000; a scan or name search not on file at the register, in receiving or in Stock Count finds it there: Restore (with its details), Write off (count only), or just look; a count ends with a "found and restored" list; (3) *trim candidates*: items with no sales since a chosen date / never sold, with on hand and last received, reviewed by brand/department; (4) sales history feeds the Movement screen (same weeks last year, seasonality); (5) ILSA vendors and any target margins. *Beta tie-out*: the owner will check ViewTouch reports against ILSA printouts during the BH beta to prove accuracy (and expects to find other accounting/data issues): a side-by-side report for a day or range (sales by department, tax, tenders, discounts, voids/refunds, gift cards, item counts) with a variance column, for imported ILSA days (checks the importer) and parallel beta days.
* **BH coupons and manufacturer coupons as tenders (owner 2026-11-04; not started)**: BH coupon: a customer brings 10 carton UPCs (cut from cartons; no barcode of its own) and gets \$2.50 off a carton; the cashier decides eligibility (no scan check of the UPCs). Taken as a TENDER, like a manufacturer coupon (no change to taxable sales), as in ILSA (dept 440 BUTTHEADS COUPON / 460 MFG COUPONS history). Build: a "BH Coupon" tender fixed at \$2.50 (several per sale allowed) and an "MFG Coupon" tender with the amount typed; each BH coupon used prints its own redemption slip for auditing ("BH COUPON REDEMPTION \$2.50", date, receipt #, carton sold, cashier, room to staple the 10 UPCs); coupon counts and \$ per day/month in reports and the ILSA tie-out. Manufacturer coupon barcodes (GS1 DataBar, AI 8110): USB handheld scanners read them (DS9808 does), the tablet camera (ML Kit) can't; parse the offer code, cashier confirms the amount.
* **ILSA import: what the BH Danbury data is and the cutover plan (owner 2026-11-04)**: Source: `copy bhdan 10-29-2026.mdb` (owner's Google Drive, Neil's folder; on the Pi in ~/Downloads; CSVs in ~/ilsa-export/, never in the repo). History 2014-03-21..2026-10-29, one register, ~4,600 daily Z's, 415,506 receipts (RegSale; key REG_NUM+RECEIPT_NUM unique), 855,867 receipt lines (PDetail rows that match a receipt), 402,509 tenders (all match). Owner: older (2009-2014) data may come later; no receiving was done in ILSA; only sales and movement data matter; receipts carry no customer data, only the buyer's DOB (AGE_VERIFIED: 18 before 2019-10-01, 21 after). *Changed 2026-11-06 (owner decision): import the buyer's DOB with each legacy receipt too, and print it on legacy receipts when recalled, as ILSA did. The history DB stays on the Pi (never in the repo, which is public; tests use made-up dates).* Line codes (PDetail DETAIL_CLASS,DETAIL_TYPE): 1,1 item sales (+$17.48M); 1,6 negative dept entries (lottery/online redemptions, comp lottery, mfg coupons; -$468k); 15,<PRC_ADJ id> promotions/buydowns (cigarettes; -$74k); 16,<id> small promo reversals; 30,30 unknown (+$303k, 7,351 lines; check against Z); 4,1 dept-0 entries (= MDetail class 4, $129k); 9,15 not on receipts (= InvAdj type 15, ILSA stock moves): skip. MODE_FLAGS: 1/128 pairs = voided lines (net 0); 256 = returns; 4096, 32768 to confirm. RegSale Z_MODE: 0 normal ($17.8M), 1 = 4,497 receipts $306,592 (unknown: training? check with vt-ilsa-tieout), 2 = 11,590 receipts at $0. Tenders: 1 Cash, 10 Personal Check, 15 BH Gift Card, 70 Credit Cards. Tax: STANDARD 6.35%. Trim estimate (year of last PLU sale): 2026 4,378; 2025 2,250; 2024 1,667; 2023 1,464; 2019-2022 ~4,200; 2014-2018 ~4,100; never sold 15,479 (keep items with stock on hand regardless). ILSA's own Z numbers (Z_Totals/Z_Tender/Z_Taxes) let the importer check itself on every day. Lottery: BH stopped selling lottery over 3 years ago (owner): lottery departments (270-405, 420 comp lottery) import as history only, never set up for selling; their items fall into the trim list; movement/reorder reports leave them out. *Parallel run (owner)*: during the beta BH rings the same sales in ILSA and ViewTouch to test transaction behavior (cards charged on one system only; gift cards redeemed on one only; daily compare ILSA Z vs ViewTouch end of day); beta ViewTouch data is wiped at cutover. *Self-check findings (vt-ilsa-tieout 1-4, 2026-11-04)*: tax matches ILSA's Z on 4,575 of 4,583 days; TendSale SALE_AMT is the whole sale repeated on each tender row (never sum it as the tender); tender = AMT_TEND: cards 4,583/4,583 days, checks 338/338, gift cards 386/386 to the cent; cash = cash AMT_TEND - CHANGE_DUE matches 3,861 days; ILSA also takes change given on card/gift overtenders out of cash (explains ~139 more days). Of the \$235,651 total cash gap, \$230,003.00 is ONE 2023 day (ILSA's Z cash inflated: likely a mistyped amount); the other ~580 days are small (-\$280..+\$1,083), consistent with the owner's ILSA crash days (one journal didn't write to the other): import receipts as they are and list those days on a "Known ILSA differences" report. Paid outs: MDetail class 4 = PDetail 4,1 lines = RegReconcile Z_PAYOUTS (\$18,496.56 on the gap days; \$128,917 in all): import as paid outs (not sales, not tenders). Receipt modes 1 (\$306,592, no tenders) and 2 (\$0) are not sales (likely cancelled sales / no sales; ILSA's Z counters for these are all 0). *Source files (owner 2026-11-05: use these to reconstruct the history)*: on the Pi in ~/Downloads: `copy bhdan 10-29-2026.mdb` (2014-03-21..2026-10-29), `ILSA.mdb_CopyDB_jan2006_to_fall2008` (2006-12-10..2011-08-02), `Copy of ILSA.mdb` (740 MB: 2008-11-06..2012-12-13, 239,608 receipts; 2009 and 2010 identical to the 2006-2011 file, adds 2011-08..2012-12-13), `ILSA - Copy.mdb` (960 MB: 2008-11-06..2013-07-23, 267,086 receipts; contains everything in `Copy of ILSA.mdb` plus 2012-12-14..2013-07-23). `ILSA-kpbhdanmissing.mdb` (1.1 GB: 2008-11-06..2015-01-24; 2009-2012 identical to the others; fills 2013-07-24..2014-03-20; overlaps copy bhdan 2014-03-21..2015-01-24): CONFIRMED (vt-ilsa-compare 2026-11-05): all four files are BUTTHEAD'S TOBACCO EMPORIUM store 1, DANBURY, CT; every shared day identical (kp vs copy bhdan: 308/308; kp vs ILSA - Copy: 1,709/1,710, the exception 2013-07-23 = the copy was made mid-day; kp vs 2006 file: 992/992). *Import from (one source per day, no gaps)*: 2006-12-10..2008-11-05 `ILSA.mdb_CopyDB_jan2006_to_fall2008`; 2008-11-06..2014-03-20 `ILSA-kpbhdanmissing.mdb`; 2014-03-21..2026-10-29 (and the fresh copy at cutover) `copy bhdan 10-29-2026.mdb`. About 836,000 receipts. `ILSA - Copy.mdb` is not needed (inside kp). *Owner 2026-11-05: retain the completed history; at import time he provides the latest ILSA copy and Bob merges its up-to-date data.* Plan: build the history once into ~/ilsa-history/ilsa-history.db (SQLite on the Pi, never in the repo: receipts, lines, tenders, paid outs, items, departments, ILSA Z's; each receipt tagged with its source file) with its self-check report; at cutover merge the latest copy: for every day the latest copy has, its data replaces the stored day (a copy's last day can be partial, e.g. 2013-07-23), then import the whole history into ViewTouch. Not used: `ilsa.mdb` (2.9 MB, empty), `Copy of ILSA.mdb` (subset of `ILSA - Copy.mdb`), `PosReceipts.mdb` (ILSA's rolling last-7-days receipt text for register reprints; has a PAN column: never import). The importer takes all of them, labels each receipt with its source database, and skips receipts that appear in more than one. *Older database (2026-11-05)*: `ILSA.mdb_CopyDB_jan2006_to_fall2008` (1.2 GB, on the Pi in ~/Downloads; CSVs in ~/ilsa-export/ILSA) actually holds 2006-12-10..2011-08-02: 292,278 receipts (\$7.57M), 644,524 lines, 285,228 tenders, one register, same table layout and codes. No overlap with the 2014 database (gap 2011-08-03..2014-03-20 still missing). Receipt numbers may repeat across databases: key imported receipts by source database + register + receipt #. Same rules hold: checks 1,248/1,248 days, gift cards 92/92, cards (70) 1,190/1,190 to the cent with AMT_TEND; cash AMT_TEND - CHANGE_DUE off by only \$8,042 in total (one mistyped huge tendered amount makes raw AMT_TEND useless for cash). Extra tender types then: 40 OLD CC (to 2010-03-16, then 70), 80 American Express, 12 House Charge Account, 0 (160 rows, Dec 2009): map 40/80 to card, 12 to house charge. *Cutover plan (owner)*: about two weeks of checking until the data is clean; then wipe ViewTouch and import the good data from a fresh ILSA copy. *Changed 2026-11-06 (owner approved): at cutover wipe only the beta's sales and transactions; KEEP the item list (with the names, sizes, makers and groups fixed in ViewTouch); the importer adds only items new in ILSA (matched by UPC, names left alone) and imports the sales history onto the kept items; the 15-minute price sync updates prices by UPC only, never names.* So the importer must be a repeatable script (export -> import -> self-check), run with ViewTouch stopped, with a backup of viewtouch.db first. *Owner 2026-11-07 (check numbering):* ViewTouch checks use the store prefixes HCA, BHD (Danbury) and BHG (Gaylordsville) with 6-digit numbers starting at 100001 (e.g. BHD-100001; Store Settings > Receipts > Check prefix, the wipe/cutover step sets each store's next check number to 100001; after 999999 numbers simply grow to 7 digits). ILSA history keeps its own prefixes BHDAN and BHNM, so old and new numbers never collide and old receipts stay found under their old numbers. *Owner 2026-11-07 (transition checklist):* (1) ILSA history is in (2006-12-10..2026-10-29, three copies; ~/ilsa-history/ilsa-history.db). At cutover: export the final ILSA copy and run `vt-ilsa-history <final export> --from 2026-10-29` so it picks up from the last day imported (2026-10-29 is kept as partial and replaced). (2) Wipe every ViewTouch transaction made during the beta (the owner's test checks, payments, drawers, time clock tests), keeping items, settings, staff, pages and the ILSA history file. Needs a 'wipe beta transactions' step with a backup first (not built yet). (3) Parallel testing until then: the owner rings a selection of transactions in ViewTouch alongside the ILSA register, not every sale, so whole days won't match; the goal is to try many kinds of transactions (voids, returns, discounts, gift cards, split tenders, age checks, buydowns) and check ViewTouch's behavior and reports transaction by transaction.
* **Tablet kiosk: Bluetooth pairing and "Close ViewTouch" (found 2026-11-05)**: opening Android Bluetooth settings from the Pi (adb am start android.settings.BLUETOOTH_SETTINGS) fails with error 101 (lock task mode). Pairing the Unitech MS912+ needed Manager > Close ViewTouch, and after that a reboot did NOT bring ViewTouch back (Kiosk.leave clears the HOME preference and disables the Home component; KioskBoot's start from BOOT_COMPLETED is blocked as a background start); fixed by starting QtActivity with adb or tapping the icon (Kiosk.apply re-locks). To do in the next APK: a "Bluetooth scanner" button (Site Setup > Tablets, and on the tablet) that temporarily allows com.android.settings in setLockTaskPackages and opens Bluetooth settings, then returns; and keep HOME so a reboot after Close ViewTouch returns to ViewTouch.
* **ILSA price book sync (built 2026-11-05; automatic in ViewTouch since 2026-11-07)**: *Now automatic (owner 2026-11-07, branch bob/ilsa-sync): the Pi's ViewTouch (the one serving the store, vt-server --serve) runs vt-ilsa-pricebook --exit-status every 15 minutes (first run a minute after start); when a new export came in (or the latest wasn't synced yet: ~/ilsa-sync/last-synced.txt) it runs vt-ilsa-items and applies ~/ilsa-items/viewtouch-menu.json with PosService::syncPriceBook: new ILSA items ADDED (same conversion rules, into their department; id ilsa-<PLU>); PRICES of existing items follow ILSA, and since 2026-11-07 (branch bob/audit) so do COSTS: vt-ilsa-items sends PLU.LAST_COST (the owner's system cost, list + OTP, kept in ILSA; ILSA copies it into each sale line's ITEM_COST) as the item's cost, new items get it, an existing item's cost follows it (logged 'cost old -> new'), an empty ILSA cost never clears a ViewTouch cost; each sale line freezes its cost when rung, so a cost change never alters past sales; new barcodes ILSA has for an item are added; NAMES, sizes, category, tax and everything else set in ViewTouch never change (ILSA name / department / tax differences and items gone from ILSA are only logged, once each); items are matched by ANY barcode, then by the ilsa-<PLU> id, never by name; an item two ILSA items point at keeps its price when they disagree (logged); nothing merged or deleted. No login needed (Changes report: "ILSA sync"); one menu revision per run; open checks keep their prices. Logs in ~/ilsa-sync: sync-log.csv, runs.log, price-changes-<time>.csv. Site Setup > ILSA price book: On/Off (Store Settings' ilsaSyncOn, default on), last run, Sync now, Show log. Menu Builder > Import a menu is no longer needed for the price book.* The ILSA Windows 7 PC (Samsung Slate Pro, C:\ilsa\data\ilsacorp.mdb) runs "ViewTouch ILSA Sync" every 15 minutes (tools/ilsa-windows; Jet text export, seconds) and sends PLU, PRC_DATA, PRC_NAME, Inv_Info, Dept, Dept_Grp, Pos_Flag to Dropbox\POS project\ilsa-pricebook only when they change; the Pi pulls with scripts/bin/vt-ilsa-pricebook. Neil's "ILSA Price book sync" task (every 15 min, failing since 11-03) to be deleted by the owner. First book: 33,552 items; PLU.LAST_PRICE equals the PRC_DATA price on every item (one price per item). The ACTIVE flag is useless (owner: ignore it; ILSACorp shows 7,327 active / 26,225 inactive): Owner 2026-11-05: import ALL ILSA items (lots of old stock); do NOT import quantities on hand (movement comes from the history); wants what sold at what price and GP on cost at the time CONFIRMED (vt-ilsa-costcheck 2026-11-05): each ILSA sale line (PDetail 1/1) has unit price DETAIL_ITEM_PRC, quantity DETAIL_FOR_QTY (DETAIL_AT_QTY unused), unit cost at the time ITEM_COST (AVG_COST empty); line cost = ITEM_COST x DETAIL_FOR_QTY. Merchandise GP (lottery out, no-cost lines out): 2014 26.9%, 2015-2019 29-30%, 2020-2022 30-31%, 2023-2026 32.0-32.2%, matching the owner's expectation (~25% rising to 30-32%). Lines without a cost are almost all dept 260 GM OTHER GM (\$2k-\$75k a year): import as "no cost", left out of GP and shown separately. Lottery: instant tickets (270, 275) carry cost (5% GP); online lottery depts have none; lottery sales ended in 2023; kept as their own departments, out of merchandise GP.
* **33,000-item catalog speed (test run 42112495087, CI machine, 2026-11-05)**: import via importMenuFile 1.1 s; item list built 0.19 s (0.34 s per later call: copied each time); save all 0.5 s; load 0.15 s: fine. NOT fine: (1) a barcode scan takes 0.36 s (LayoutController::scanned walks every item's QVariantMap and splits its barcodes string; ~1 s on the Pi); (2) the item list is 26.7 MB as JSON and is synced whole to every tablet (menuItems, Group::Admin) on each menu change, so every 15-minute price sync would push ~27 MB to each tablet. Before importing the ILSA price book: (a) a code -> item index on the server (hash, rebuilt when the menu changes) used by scans everywhere (tablets ask the server); (b) tablets get a slim item list (no 33,000 retail items; search and labels ask the server). Also add manufacturer / product group / sub group to MenuItem so ViewTouch keeps the ILSA tree (the Labels tree uses the ILSA price book today).
* **Departments going forward (owner 2026-11-05)**: of Newspapers, Magazines, Beverages, Candy, Food, Vapor, Event Tickets, only MAGAZINES and EVENT TICKETS continue. VAPOR is eliminated now (owner 2026-11-05): vt-ilsa-items leaves departments 55/56 out (history only), like lottery. The others (and lottery) are history only; the owner deletes them "at the appropriate time" (import them as they are for now; retire later, history keeps them). vt-ilsa-items found 31,906 items in 22 categories (lottery's 1,646 left out), 558 same-name groups (1,161 items), 8,745 items on the owner's 708694 codes (4,175 never sold since 2014; 72 with a same-name maker-UPC twin).
* **Owner 2026-11-05: NO automatic merges; in-house UPCs; history follows UPC changes**: every ILSA item imports as its own ViewTouch item; the owner decides which UPCs to merge (vt-ilsa-items writes possible-duplicates.csv for review). 708694xxxxxx is the owner's own GS1 UPC range: he wants to free as many as possible (inhouse-upcs.csv: in-house codes, last sale, same-name items with a maker's UPC = candidates). History must follow later merges/re-codes "retroactively": keep every sale line's original UPC untouched and add a UPC map (code, from date, to date -> ViewTouch item) maintained by the merge / re-code actions; reports and movement read history through the map. Date ranges matter because a freed in-house code reused for a new product must not pull the old product's sales onto the new one. *Retiring items and reusing in-house codes (owner 2026-11-05)*: an item with sales history is never hard-deleted: Retire hides it from selling, search, labels and reorder; its history stays; scanning one of its codes offers Restore (items with no history can be deleted outright). Retiring releases its 708694 codes: the UPC map entry gets an end date and the code goes to a free-code pool (only after a cool-off since its last sale, owner's choice, e.g. 12 months, and never while ViewTouch thinks stock is on the shelf); a new item can take the next free in-house code (check digit worked out; labels from the Labels screen); sales before the end date stay with the old item, after it with the new one. *Full-store physical inventory (owner 2026-11-05; process TO BE AGREED with the owner, based on what's selling)*: most in-house 708694 codes date from years ago when many makers didn't barcode; the owner will review the legacy UPCs, and a scan of the entire store will clear them up. Likely shape (not decided): every item on the shelf scanned and counted; afterwards ViewTouch lists in-house codes NOT found and not sold since a chosen date (retire, code freed), in-house items found that now have a maker's barcode (relabel or merge, history follows through the UPC map), and items found but not on file (restore from the ILSA reference or add). *Tree restructuring the same way (owner 2026-11-05)*: history lines also keep the department / manufacturer / product group / sub group as sold; reports group history by the item's CURRENT tree (code -> item through the UPC map -> its department and brand today), so moving items between departments, splitting or regrouping departments, or renaming a manufacturer re-groups all past sales automatically; plus name maps (e.g. SWED MATCH -> SWEDISH MATCH, old department -> new) for codes no longer on any item; an "as recorded then" view stays available for the ILSA tie-out.
* **ILSA cleanup: duplicate items and box sizes (owner 2026-11-04; part of the ILSA import)**: (1) *Duplicates*: alt UPC never worked in ILSA, so many products exist as several items with different UPCs. During the import, find likely duplicates (same brand/size/price, names alike after normalizing abbreviations, items that never sold at the same time); the owner reviews groups and picks the name; the kept item gets every UPC (one main, the rest alt UPCs), the combined sales history and on hand; old receipts unchanged; merges logged and undoable until the import is final. (2) *Box sizes and box inventory* (owner sells many boxes and must know full-box inventory, so NOT a single stick total): each cigar keeps separate on-hand counts: sealed boxes per size (20, 24... each size has its own unique UPC, so a scan always knows) and loose singles. Receiving a box: that size's sealed +1; box sale: sealed -1; single sale: loose -1. *Open a box*: cashiers tap it whenever they open one (owner wants it done then): pick the size, sealed -1, loose +20/24. Loose can be more than one box (e.g. a 10-count box with 12 on the shelf so a 3-5 cigar sale never leaves 2): no limit on loose. Fallback when a single sale would take loose below 0: "No loose singles on hand. Did you open a box? [Box of 20] [Box of 24] [Not now]" (doesn't hold up the sale). Recording doesn't have to happen at the shelf: an *EMPTIED A BOX* button on the Cashier Duty screen (retail mode) opens the box-opening screen; scan each empty box's barcode (its UPC gives cigar and size), several in one go ("empty box back to the counter, scan it, toss it"); a humidor attendant at HCA switches a tablet to retail mode and does it there (tablet camera Scan works). Optional per-item *shelf minimum* for loose singles: a reminder to open a box when below it. Reports: box inventory (sealed by brand/size, value), movement split into boxes sold / singles sold / boxes opened, reorder in boxes; Stock Count counts sealed per size and loose separately (differences show as a missing box vs missing singles). Total sticks only for valuation.
* **ID scan for age-restricted sales (first version built 2026-11-04; refine with the owner when BH Danbury runs beta beside ILSA later in November)**: Scan the license barcode (PDF417/AAMVA) on each tobacco, cigarette, rolling-paper, e-cig and alcohol sale; block under the Store Settings > Age-restricted sales minimums (ageTobacco / ageAlcohol, already there) or an expired ID; log only "ID checked, 21+, who, when" on the check. The owner wants an ID from every tobacco buyer, so a guest-screen PIN can't replace the ID. The owner has more requirements for this, including IDs whose barcode won't read or that have none: ask him before starting. Needs a 2D scanner.
* **Upstream sync ledger (official ViewTouch: github.com/ViewTouch/viewtouch)**: the fork's base is d9ad3d0a (2026-10-15). Policy (owner left it to Bob, 2026-11-04): stay a downstream fork; bring upstream fixes in small groups, each tested on GitHub (Debian 13, Qt 6.8) before the Pi; record each upstream id in the Log; ~~no Qt 6.12 work while the Pi runs Qt 6.8~~ replaced: owner 2026-11-13: move to Qt 6.12 before BHDAN goes live (branch helper/qt612; Pi plan in modern/docs/QT612_MOVE.md; the code keeps building on 6.8 until every Pi has moved; both Pis now run the official Qt 6.12.0 from /opt/qt/6.12.0: HCA since 2026-11-13, BHDAN confirmed by the owner 2026-11-25 with vt-qt612 status); offer good general fixes of ours back upstream. * **4-Node Multi-Store Hub & Web Portal**: `store_config.json` and `corp_sync.py` across `CORP_HUB`, `HCAH_DANBURY`, `BHT_DANBURY`, and `BHT_GAYLORDSVILLE`, plus InMotion PHP/MySQL online ordering with Pi background polling and ESC/POS kitchen auto-printing (`192.168.1.77:9100`).
* **Standby / failover as an option (owner 2026-11-08)**: today a server that finds another server already running the SAME store (same serverId) turns itself into that store's standby automatically (main.cpp findMainServer -> stepDown). Different stores never interfere (each has its own serverId), but a copied Pi kept the original's id and became Danbury's standby (fixed in vt-copy-store: the copy gets a new identity). Wanted: a site setting (vt-site / Hardware Configuration) 'This computer may become a standby: Yes / No' (No for every site Pi that's its own store; Yes only on a machine meant as a backup), and when No, a clear refusal on screen ('Another computer is already running this store at <host>') instead of silently becoming its standby; plus Manager > Network on the main showing and removing standbys. Also: vt-server sometimes ends after the build without starting ViewTouch (seen on .225 2026-11-08; vt-start works): find why.
* **Receipt design: revisit later (owner 2026-11-08: satisfied for now)**: done so far: customer details off by default (Store Settings > Receipts > Customer on receipts: None / First name / Name, phone and address; kitchen and pickup tickets keep the name), short privacy notice centered in the printer's small font (Font B), receipt barcode and check prefix options, the store logo on receipts (option), ILSA receipts with the store header. To look at again: an in-between size for the privacy notice (TM-T88IV has only Font A 12x24 and Font B 9x17; in-between means printing it as a picture), the overall receipt layout and spacing, what prints at the top (logo size for older printers), and the sample/test receipt's wording (still shows restaurant items).
* **SMS texting and customer follow-ups (owner 2026-11-07, idea)**: Today: Store Settings > Waitlist and texts > Texting service URL (POSTs JSON {to, message}); used by the waitlist Notify (editable 'table-ready text', {name}/{store}) and texted receipts (fixed wording). Planned: (1) a relay on the Pi for Twilio (account keys only on the Pi, never in the repo); (2) every text editable in Store Settings as a template with fields ({name}, {store}, {check}, {total}, {event}, {date}, {balance}, {link}); (3) customer sign-up by cell phone at the register, with a follow-up text asking for their email for the weekly events list (reply with the email, or a link to a short form); (4) per-customer text choices recorded with date and how given: receipts/notices, events and offers, or none; (5) event reminders: pick an event, a message and the customers who chose event texts (by store, subscription, last visit), send now or schedule; (6) opt-outs: a STOP reply is caught by Twilio and sent back to the relay, which marks the customer 'no texts' at once (shown on their record, with the date), and every later send skips them; START turns it back on; (7) a texts report: sent, delivered, failed, opt-outs, by event. Ties into the cross-store customer list (phone is the key) and the HCA subscription program. *Phone changes (owner 2026-11-07)*: the customer record keeps its own id, so history, points, subscription and gift cards stay with the person; editing the phone keeps the old number on the record as 'previous' (not used for lookups or texts once someone else has it; a scan of a receipt/card still finds the person); text choices belong to the number: the new number starts with event texts off until the customer says yes again, a STOP on the old number stays with the old number; if the new number already belongs to another record, offer to merge the two; the change is logged (who, when) and syncs to every store.
* **One customer across all stores (owner 2026-11-07, planned with the corporate Pi)**: a customer is the same person at BH Danbury, HCA and any future site. One shared customer list kept by the corporate Pi, with a copy on every site Pi so lookups and new customers work when the VPN is down (synced when it's back). Match key: the cell phone number (owner's choice); staff confirm the name; two records with the same phone are merged (history follows). At any register (bar or store), the customer's Past transactions / Past items show purchases from every location, newest first, each marked with its store (e.g. BH Danbury, HCA); *Owner 2026-11-07 16:57: a synchronized transaction file across all stores with a check prefix*: every check number carries its store's prefix (e.g. BH-104233, HCA-5012) so numbers never collide; each store writes only its own checks, and closed checks are copied (read-only) through the corporate Pi to every other store, so any register can look up and reprint another store's check offline; a sale is never changed at a store that didn't ring it (returns of another store's sale are rung as a new check that points to the original). ILSA history has no customer on its receipts, so purchases from before ViewTouch can't be tied to a person: for those, look up by item, UPC, date or total on Look Up Checks > ILSA history. Fits the HCA subscription program (pricing at bar and retail).
* **One customer across all stores (owner 2026-11-07, planned with the corporate Pi)**: a customer is the same person at BH Danbury, HCA and any future site. One shared customer list kept by the corporate Pi, with a copy on every site Pi so lookups and new customers work when the VPN is down (synced when it's back). Match key: the cell phone number (owner's choice); staff confirm the name; two records with the same phone are merged (history follows). At any register (bar or store), the customer's Past transactions / Past items show purchases from every location, newest first, each marked with its store (e.g. BH Danbury, HCA); *Owner 2026-11-07 16:57: a synchronized transaction file across all stores with a check prefix*: every check number carries its store's prefix (HCA Hat City Ale House, BHDAN Butthead's Danbury, BHNM Butthead's New Milford = Gaylordsville, dormant since its last ILSA sale in 2024-09, which the owner hopes to reopen on ViewTouch as its own site; e.g. BHDAN-104233, HCA-5012) so numbers never collide; the imported ILSA history already shows its receipts this way (BHDAN-836100: legacy_sources.store, set by vt-ilsa-history --prefix); each store writes only its own checks, and closed checks are copied (read-only) through the corporate Pi to every other store, so any register can look up and reprint another store's check offline; a sale is never changed at a store that didn't ring it (returns of another store's sale are rung as a new check that points to the original). ILSA history has no customer on its receipts, so purchases from before ViewTouch can't be tied to a person: for those, look up by item, UPC, date or total on Look Up Checks > ILSA history. Fits the HCA subscription program (pricing at bar and retail).
* **Temporary stand-in until then (2026-11-26, Bob (Claude) helper): customers shared store to store** — Store Settings > Other stores > *Share customers*; `viewtouch-customers` v1 files through the transfers outbox; merged by phone digits, newest change per field wins, a clearly different name waits on Manager > Customers > Check These Customers; house accounts, points, visits stay local. **Remove when the corporate customer list exists**: `app/pos_customer_sync.cpp`, `core/customer_sync.hh`, `ui/qml/CustomerSyncPanel.qml`, `tests/test_customer_sync.cpp` and the lines marked temporary (list in docs/MASS_UPDATES_STORES.md).
* **Corporate ViewTouch & shared gift cards (planned, to be implemented later)**: The owner will build a Corporate ViewTouch
  instance on a separate Pi before the Buttheads stores launch. Then: one shared card list so balances work at all three
  stores (Hat City Ale House; Buttheads Tobacco Emporium - Danbury; Buttheads Tobacco Emporium - Gaylordsville), and a
  per-terminal "This terminal's store" setting that overrides Store Settings > Gift card location (one shared Store Settings
  can't tell the stores apart). Until then each store's own Gift card location is used.
* **Mobile ID age check (owner 2026-11-20, waiting on the state)**: verify a customer's mobile driver's license
  (Connecticut Mobile ID, launched 2026-11-17; Google/Apple/Samsung Wallet IDs and other states later) in the age check,
  showing only "21+ Verified" (green) or a red reason; nothing about the person is stored, only that the check passed,
  kept on the check like today's ID check. Standard: ISO/IEC 18013-5 (QR code engagement + Bluetooth LE; tablets have
  no NFC, QR is enough). Plan: the free open-source Multipaz reader (OpenWallet Foundation, recommended by Google) in
  the Android app. Tablets (HCA) read directly; the Pi registers (BHDAN, Gaylordsville) get a small Android device
  paired to the register in an "ID reader" mode whose result lands on that register's check (option 1, chosen first).
  Later option 2: read the QR with the register's 2D scanner and do the Bluetooth part on the Pi itself (no Multipaz
  for Linux: our own reader, bigger job). Blocked until: (a) CT DMV answers whether it publishes its Mobile ID issuer
  (IACA) certificate or joins AAMVA's Digital Trust Service so readers other than IDEMIA's can verify it (owner emailed
  2026-11-20); if not, the only path is IDEMIA's Verify SDK (ask IDEMIA for price and license terms); (b) the state's
  consumer protection department (DCP) says whether Mobile ID is acceptable for tobacco sales. Meanwhile staff can
  use IDEMIA's free Mobile ID Verify app on a phone.
  Alternative to weigh first (owner 2026-11-20, We Card eNews): **TruAge** (NACS's age verification, free to POS
  companies and retailers; works with existing 2D scanners; reads U.S. driver's licenses and state mobile licenses that
  integrate TruAge; logs proof of each check; integrated by Verifone, Gilbarco, NCR Voyix, Clover app, Pinnacle,
  Success Systems, Minor Decliner, Tenderfoot). If ViewTouch can integrate it, the Pi registers need no extra device.
  Open: how a POS joins (API/SDK/certification), whether tobacco shops and bars qualify, whether CT Mobile ID works with
  it, and whether it needs the internet. Contact: support@mytruage.org.
* **Evaluated Upstream Candidates** (commit hashes from the upstream ViewTouch repository, not in this fork): `3f080bc0` (scan from any screen), `9c8a315d` (`v0.10-ui-audit` button fit), `c4b87db0` & `5cad9b09` (offline tablet selling & single-round offline kits), and `178c745e` & `222aeaf4` (peer-to-peer OTA tablet APK streaming).

## Log

### 2026-11-27 17:19 ET — Bob (Claude) helper — Receive retail: allowance per unit on stock lines (net cost, Allowances on Purchases, allowance rows in invoice files)
- **What:** Tablet app: changed. Server: changed. Stock lines get an Allowance per unit (typed positive, beside OTP / Prepaid tax): net cost per unit = price + OTP - allowance (prepaid tax still not cost); the line total and the tie-out take it off (2 @ 131.97 - 2 x .60 = 262.74). Cost Diffs compares the net cost and shows the allowance per unit in a new Allowance column; the vendor's last cost keeps the price (lastCost) and the allowance (lastAllowance), lastUnitCents() is the net. Retail Purchases: new Allowances column (vendor group / vendor / invoice / total), Stock stays gross, the header note says so. Invoice files: an Allowance column (per unit), and an allowance row (no item # / UPC, description contains allowance) goes onto the item row directly above it (per unit from its unit price, else extension / that item's qty), never a line of its own; qty 0 rows listed, add nothing. Reopened invoices: allowance editable, no stock moves. Tests (core and app), es/fr/pt/zh, manual ch. 35 'Allowances on the invoice'.
- **Why:** The owner's distributor invoice has '** Allowance on Above Item' lines (an off-invoice manufacturer allowance) that lower what he pays; without them the tie-out was off and Cost Diffs showed the gross price.
- **Files:** `modern/app/pos_receiving.cpp`, `modern/core/receiving.hh`, `modern/docs/manual/manual.html`, `modern/i18n/es.json`, `modern/i18n/fr.json`, `modern/i18n/pt.json`, `modern/i18n/zh.json`, `modern/tests/test_receiving_core.cpp`, `modern/tests/test_retail_receiving.cpp`, `modern/ui/qml/WidgetRetailReceiving.qml`
- **Next person must know:** Line JSON and vendor_items JSON carry allowance / lastAllowance only when nonzero (old records read the same). An allowance row with no item row above it stays a row (a credit).

### 2026-11-27 15:47 ET — Bob (Claude) helper — Receive retail: Save for later, Unfinished invoices (autosaved), Receive asks when off; reopen a received invoice (only the difference on hand)
- **What:** Tablet app: changed (Receive retail screen). Server: changed. Receive invoice now has two actions: Save for later (kept as unfinished, nothing on hand) and Receive (stock added; asks 'Off by $x - receive anyway?' or when no total was typed). The invoice on the screen is autosaved on every change in a new table retail_drafts (survives restarts/updates; a screen left with one finds it again), listed on a new Unfinished tab (vendor, invoice #, date, lines, units, who, last changed when/where) with Open and Delete (asked). One screen at a time: another screen sees 'being edited at X' and can 'Open here anyway' (takes it over; the old screen's next change isn't kept). New invoice keeps the one on the screen on Unfinished. Invoices received > Open shows the lines and the change history; Edit reopens it: Save changes applies only the DIFFERENCE to on hand per item (carton lines as sealed cartons, packs loose), keeps an edit record (who, when, terminal, totals/units before and after, each changed line), re-learns the vendor's last cost (newer-or-same invoice date only), and Purchases/Cost Diffs show it as it is now ('(changed)' on Purchases). A received invoice keeps its vendor; changes made elsewhere since it was opened are refused. Duplicate vendor + invoice # as a new invoice: Receive offers 'already received - open it to add these lines?' (also a banner as soon as the number is typed). Commands recvSave -> recvReceive(anyway) / recvSaveForLater / recvConfirmCancel / recvOpenDraft / recvDeleteDraft / recvShowReceived / recvEditReceived / recvAddToReceived. Tests: autosave reload from the store, Save for later, nototal/off confirm, holds between screens, reopen + carton line difference, line taken off, duplicate offer, UI Save for later -> Unfinished -> Open -> Receive. es/fr/pt/zh for the 61 new phrases; manual chapter 35.
- **Why:** Owner pressed Save meaning 'save my work' before an invoice was finished: it was received at once and the same invoice # could never be received again. Work in memory was also lost on every restart (updates restart ViewTouch).

### 2026-11-27 13:15 ET — Bob (Claude) helper — Cartons and boxes on hand: sealed + loose, pack sales open a carton by themselves (branch helper/carton-stock)
- **What:** Tablet app: changed (Menu Builder item card gets On hand: Count sealed + loose, Open boxes; Units on the Parent/Child row; Transfers line has cartons + loose packs fields; Receive retail line says = 2 ctn / = 1 pk; new session key retailStockCard). Server: changed. A box / carton and its single (the single's __parent__ link) are one family with two counts, kept in retail_stock as before: sealed on the box's item row, loose on the single's. Shown on both items as '12 ctn 3 pk' / '4 box 27 ea' (units: the single's new __units__:<box>/<single> recipe tag, else box/ea for cigars, ctn/pk otherwise). Rules (core/retail_stock.hh): a single sold takes 1 loose; only with none loose a sealed one opens by itself (sealed -1, loose + pack - 1), logged; a box sold takes 1 sealed, with none sealed a box's worth from loose (logged), else sealed below 0 (logged); voids/returns back as sold; receiving a carton (UPC or vendor unit of whole cartons) adds sealed, a pack loose; transfers: one line cartons + loose packs, sent the same way as a sale, voided back as sent, a carton received as packs at a store with the carton linked goes sealed; Open N boxes moves sealed to loose; counts typed sealed + loose apart (managers, logged). Value at cost = sealed x box cost + loose x single cost. Items without a link keep their own count. Once at start: counts kept before settled (a carton below 0 made up from whole cartons loose; for cigarettes whole cartons loose back to sealed; cigars stay loose), logged on Changes, marker row __families_v2__ in retail_stock. New app/pos_retail_stock.cpp, core/retail_stock.hh, ui/qml/MenuBuilderStock.qml, tests/test_carton_stock.cpp; test_transfers updated for the shared carton+pack line. Translations es/fr/pt/zh (25 phrases). Manual: ch. 35 new section 'Cartons, boxes and loose singles' (two worked examples), cross-references from ch. 34 transfers and Counting stock.
- **Why:** Owner 2026-11-27 (via Bob): keep carton counts, sell most by carton, pack sales break cartons automatically; cigars often have more than a box loose (Open boxes for the humidor); a manual section explaining it.

Invoices received: the list by invoice date. Reports: Retail Purchases (range by invoice date; End of Day: received today) by vendor group / vendor / invoice: stock with OTP, prepaid tax, delivery, store use, use tax, credits, other, total, and invoices that don't tie out; Retail Cost Diffs: invoice cost per unit with OTP vs system cost per line (not history-only), a plain list. Vendors gain Vendor group (reports put a distributor's two accounts together). Tests: tests/test_receiving_core.cpp, tests/test_retail_receiving.cpp.

### 2026-11-26 19:16 ET — Bob (Claude) helper — Transfers between stores, store price flow and price zone (branch helper/store-transfers)
- **What:** Tablet app: changed (new Transfers screen, Mass Updates Price zone, Menu Builder card line, new session key transfers). Server: changed. New Manager > Inventory > Transfers… (page transfers, widget transfers, WidgetTransfers.qml; app/pos_transfers.cpp; core/transfer.hh): retail items only (itemIsRetail with a UPC), scanned / typed / Find, a quantity a line, To a store from Store Settings > Other stores, a note; Send (manager) takes them off on hand at once (new per-item retail on hand, table retail_stock, kept from an item's first transfer; sales take it down in takeStock), keeps a transfer record (table transfers, both directions), logs it on the Changes report at cost, prints a slip (Transfer #n, from/to, date, who, lines UPC/name/qty, total at cost, Received by and Signature lines) and writes a viewtouch-transfer v1 file to <data>/transfers/outbox/<STORE>/. scripts/bin/vt-send-transfers (ViewTouch starts it detached when a file is written and about once a minute while files wait; flock: one at a time) sends each file over ssh with the vt-update-stores keys into the other Pi's transfers/inbox (temp name + rename), moves it to sent/, writes send-status; addresses from transfers/stores.conf (written by ViewTouch) or OTHER_STORES. The server's ViewTouch reads its inbox every 20 s (main.cpp timer, PosService::transfersTick): a transfer waits on Receive (n); Receive: tick lines off or scan them, correct a count (sent units) with why, UPCs not on file: Add it (name/price/cost/category from the transfer) or Leave out; matched by UPC (leading zeros, 11-digit check digit) or a carton by its pack UPC x pack size; on hand up; a result file goes back and the sender's transfer shows Received / Received with differences. Void (manager, not received yet) puts stock back and sends a void (or just drops the unsent file). Offline: files wait in the outbox, the screen says Waiting to send / Couldn't reach X. New report Transfers (Reports list, range, in End of Day's reports): sent, received, in transit, differences at cost, retail stock value kept. Store Settings > Other stores (new section): This store's short name, Other stores rows (short name, ssh address, Transfers on/off, Price changes Not sent / Sent there), Take retail prices from, Its price changes are applied Automatically / When someone applies them. Price flow (one way): every change set made here (Mass Updates Apply/Undo), the Menu Builder card's price/cost save and the ILSA price book sync send retail price/cost/category changes as viewtouch-change-set files to the stores ticked Sent there; the receiving store takes them only from its Take retail prices from store, records each UPC's base price (transfers/base-prices.json), applies its price zone (PosSettings::priceZone {name, markup %, round}; Mass Updates > Price zone…: name, +%, Round None / Up to the next .49 or .99, Save, Reapply to all retail items now with preview base -> new) and applies by itself (open checks keep their prices; Changes report area Menu, from <store>) or waits on Mass Updates (Prices from X (n)…). A change set from another store is never sent on (no loops). Mass Updates grid: new '<source> price' column (sortable) at a store with a price source; Menu Builder card shows '<source> price: $x'. New Round to .49 / .99 (up) (mass::Round::Up49or99) in Mass Updates too. New command storePrice (OTHER STORE'S PRICE on the retail Discounts page, retail-discounts#rds-store-price): each retail line back to the source's base price as a discount line named '<source> price' (discount permission; allowed on No discounts items: core totals tender store-price). Tests: tests/test_transfer_core.cpp, tests/test_transfers.cpp.
- **Why:** Owner 2026-11-26: close the retail store's unprofitable hours and sell from the bar; many transfers both ways; the retail store's price book flows to the bar automatically, one way, with the bar's own zone markup; design in docs/MASS_UPDATES_STORES.md.

### 2026-11-26 17:20 ET — Bob (Claude) helper — Menu Builder: quicker Save and category moves (one refresh, no whole-menu searches)
- **What:** Tablet app: changed (QML: closed Menu Builder dialogs no longer work out their lists). Server: changed. A Menu Builder step (Save, a category moved, an import) tells the screens once when it ends, not at each menuChanged inside it (Save had every screen read everything twice; an import, once a row). The undo step's change log finds items by an index (20,000 items: 200 million compares, ~2 s). Sold out looks ingredients up in a map once. categories() keeps a set of ids. Save: What's on it saves settings (and re-totals checks) only when its group changed; a parent's new cost cascaded to its singles is now saved for them too (it was only in memory). builderItems makes only the items picked when the price book is held back, not all 20,000. The pages screen reads the sold-out list once per refresh. Closed dialogs (Before service, Change prices, Starter menus, Like...) read nothing. Slow-log parts: menu list, screen pages; category moves are timed too ('slow: move category ...').
- **Why:** Owner 2026-11-26: saving a beer's price took 5-15 s at HCA and the category arrow keys seemed dead. CI numbers before: 20,000-item price book save 2.2-2.5 s (undo 2 s), the Pi's screen refresh ~1 s, a category move 1.6 s; HCA-like 2 ms in the service.

Tests: tests/test_mass_updates.cpp (20,000 items with one page sent, find, ranges, All, previews change nothing, margin skips no-cost items, add $/%/set, cost, Changes report, Apply+Undo restores the menu exactly, export/import into a second PosService by UPC with a missing UPC, a manager can't apply / an admin can / an admin's PIN works) and a UI smoke test in tests/test_pos_flow.cpp. Phrases in es/fr/pt/zh.

### 2026-11-23 12:23 ET — Bob (Claude) helper — Which terminal rang, paid, refunded, opened and closed each sale
- **What:** Tablet app: changed (WidgetCheckSearch.qml). Server: changed. Each check now records the terminal name (the screen's name, the same one Manager > Terminals uses for After a check is paid and Has a cash drawer; the self-order kiosk: its own screen's name): OrderLine::addedOn when a line is rung (stampNewLine), Payment::takenOn when a payment is taken (stampPayment), Refund::givenOn when a refund is given, Check::openedOn when opened (startCheck, split-off checks) and Check::closedOn when it is paid, cancelled, put away, merged or discarded at End of Day (reopen clears closedOn until it closes again). Lines and payments are copied by value, so these stay through transfer, move, merge, split, reopen and void like addedBy. Saved in the check's JSON (and the refund's) only when set; no new column (the checks table stores the check as JSON); old checks load with them empty. Find a Check: 'Opened ... on X · closed ... on Y' (older checks: 'Terminal: unknown'), each line 'Rang on X', each payment 'Paid on X', each refund 'Refunded on X'. Server Sales report (also saved at End of Day, in the EOD archive PDF and the report export): a new 'By terminal (where it was paid)' section with checks, guests and net sales per terminal (closedOn; older checks '(unknown)') and 'All terminals'. Not printed on receipts. Catch2 tests in tests/test_audit_trail.cpp [terminal].
- **Why:** Owner 2026-11-23: what station rang a sale is needed. Checks recorded who rang and paid, but not on which terminal or tablet.

### 2026-11-22 14:19 ET — Bob's helper (Claude) — Tablets look after themselves; the Pi reads their reports (screens.json)
- **What:** Tablet care (new net/tablet_care.hh/.cpp, Kiosk.kt): at start, when the store comes back, when Wi-Fi comes up and every 15 min each tablet turns Wireless debugging back on (ensureWirelessAdb, also when connecting, not only at boot) and reports {grant, adb, wifiAdb, port, tlsPort, tcpPort}; asks for its update (the existing self-update; installs only while nobody is signed in); reports health (battery, charging, RSSI, uptime, restarts, reconnects); sends its own Qt log lines since the last pass (max 200). Reconnecting 3 min: reconnects from scratch (RemoteSession::reconnectFresh); 10 min with nobody signed in: restarts the app (Kiosk.restartApp, counted in SharedPreferences). Server (pos_server): keeps adbState/health per screen and what each said after it goes, appends tabletLog to tablet-logs/<screen>.log (rotated to .log.1 past 1 MB), writes screens.json beside the database (QSaveFile) on connect/disconnect/change and every minute: [{name, ip, connected, platform, appVersion, build, updateState, billboard, adb, health, lastSeen}], no keys. Manager > Network: a Debugging line and a health line under each screen. Pi: new vt-tablet-report (table + Problems line; vt-screens was already taken by the monitor tool), vt-adb-ensure goes straight to each tablet's reported port and skips the 3.5-min port scan for tablets that reported (scan is the last resort), vt-status takes versions from screens.json (adb only for a tablet not connected; else 'not connected to ViewTouch') and adds the problems line. Tests (test_net.cpp [tabletcare]); manual (Manager > Network, Tablets table), TABLET_AUTO_UPDATE.md; es/fr/pt/zh for the 11 new phrases. Tablet app: changed. Server: changed.
- **Why:** Owner test 2026-11-22 13:48 at HCA: HCA 1 (192.168.6.82, on adb 5555 before) restarted with adb reboot was back on the network in 30 s but port 5555 never reopened in 15 minutes, although vt-adb-ensure ran repeatedly (each run ~3.5 min scanning ports 30000-50000, found nothing); HCA 2 and 3 the same since their 9:16 restart; vt-status said Offline for tablets working fine in ViewTouch. Owner 14:10: 'why don't we have the tablets do a cron style routine to check for themselves and go to shared pi files for this stuff. I can't sit here every day after launch being 24/7 IT support help desk!'
- **Files:** `modern/CMakeLists.txt`, `modern/android/src/org/viewtouch/pos/Kiosk.kt`, `modern/app/pos_session.hh`, `modern/docs/TABLET_AUTO_UPDATE.md`, `modern/docs/manual/manual.html`, `modern/i18n/es.json`, `modern/i18n/fr.json`, `modern/i18n/pt.json`, `modern/i18n/zh.json`, `modern/main.cpp`, `modern/net/pos_server.cpp`, `modern/net/pos_server.hh`, `modern/net/remote_session.cpp`, `modern/net/remote_session.hh`, `modern/net/tablet_care.cpp`, `modern/net/tablet_care.hh`, `modern/tests/test_net.cpp`, `modern/ui/qml/WidgetNetwork.qml`, `scripts/bin/vt-adb-ensure`, `scripts/bin/vt-status`, `scripts/bin/vt-tablet-report`

If it shows 'Android turned it off' with the grant, suspect Android's untrusted-network rule (trusted by BSSID: a different access point counts as a new network) and the kiosk lock task hiding Android's confirm dialog. The TLS port still needs the Pi paired once in Wireless debugging. vt-tablet-report reads ~/.local/share/ViewTouch/ViewTouch/screens.json (VT_SCREENS_FILE overrides).

### 2026-11-22 11:34 ET — Bob (Claude) — Screens' port renews itself when nothing comes in; speed row's best sellers show on Qt 6.12
- **What:** net/pos_server: room for 256 pending connections, and once a minute, when no connection has come in for two minutes, the listener is closed and opened again (screens already in keep their connections); logs 'renewed the screens' port: N of M screens in' when screens are missing. WidgetSpeedRow.qml: its 'top' property renamed 'best' (Qt 6.12 refuses to override Item's FINAL 'top', so the top sellers never showed). Server: changed. Tablet app: changed.
- **Why:** HCA 2026-11-22 ~9:00-11:30: every tablet and the billboard on 'Reconnecting'; ss showed vtmodern's port 7719 with 51 connections queued (backlog 50) while ViewTouch sat idle (load 0.12, main thread in its event loop): Qt's SSL server had stopped taking connections. Restarting ViewTouch brought them back; this lets it recover by itself.
- **Files:** `modern/net/pos_server.cpp`, `modern/net/pos_server.hh`, `modern/ui/qml/WidgetSpeedRow.qml`
- **Next person must know:** Watch HCA's ~/.vt-run.log for 'renewed the screens' port'. If tablets get stuck again, run on the Pi: ss -tanp | grep 7719 (Recv-Q at the backlog = not accepting) and grab the main thread's backtrace (gdb -p PID -batch -ex 'thread 1' -ex bt) before restarting.

### 2026-11-21 12:39 ET — Bob (Claude) — TV buttons work on the tablets: the Pi runs vt-directv for every screen
- **What:** The pages' directv command (TV/DIRECTV buttons) no longer runs /usr/local/bin/vt-directv on the screen's own device. LayoutController shows 'Sending to the TV...' and calls the new PosSession::directv(box, op, val) operation, which runs on the store's main computer (PosService::directv, new modern/app/pos_tv.cpp): the Pi's own screen in-process, a tablet over the network. It checks someone is signed in (else 'Log in first.'), applies vt-directv's own argument rules (box and val ^[A-Za-z0-9_]{1,24}$, op tune or key; else refused, nothing run), runs vt-directv with an argument list (never a shell) in the background with a 10 s kill timer, and sends its one-line answer ('BAR 3: poweron sent', 'not reachable'...) back as a notice to that screen's status line only. VT_DIRECTV_LAUNCHER stands in for vt-directv (tests). PosServer now sends a terminal's notices that come on their own (after the call that started them was answered) with the next update round; before, they waited for that screen's next call. A call's own notices still go out after its reply. Tests: test_net.cpp (argument rules; tablet path with a stand-in launcher incl. not signed in, bad args, other tablet untouched, missing vt-directv; the Pi's own screen). Manual ch. 21 says the buttons work the same on every screen. Tablet app and server both changed.
- **Why:** Owner at HCA 2026-11-21: the TV/DIRECTV buttons worked on the Pi's screen but did nothing on the Android tablets (they ran vt-directv on the tablet, which hasn't got it: 'TV control isn't set up on this device').
