### 2026-11-19 15:18 ET — Bob's helper (Claude) — Retail ITEM RETURN: the old sale, or scan the item at a price the cashier may change
- **What:** New command itemReturn ('Item return (old sale or scan)' in the page editor's command list; the starter Retail page's ITEM RETURN uses it). It opens a window (ItemReturnSheet.qml; state on the main computer as pos.itemReturn, so tablets work the same): 'Find the old sale' (Find a Check, Refund... there, as before) or 'Scan or look up the item'; a scan, a typed UPC or part of a name (several: touch one) shows the item with its price-book price filled in, editable, How many (1-99) and a reason (Defective, Wrong item, Changed mind, Other). 'Add return' puts a RETURN line on the sale (PosService::addItemReturn, app/pos_returns.cpp): negative quantity at that price, the item's own tax class, never discounted, OrderLine::returnReason kept (saved, printed as 'Returned: <reason>'). Scanning a receipt barcode while the window is open goes to the old sale. Core: a tax class whose net is below zero now gets negative tax (tax-on-top and prices-include-tax), so the tax is refunded; no gratuity on a negative subtotal; return lines never discountable. Pay: a sale below zero shows 'Refund (touch Cash)'; Cash adds a negative cash payment (other tenders refuse: a card goes back via Find a Check > Refund...); Close Check refuses until the refund is given, then opens the drawer and the drawer's expected cash drops by it; receipts print '<Cash> refund'. Stock: the negative line goes back into stock at close through the same takeStock as a sale (recipe/ingredient stock). Return lines can't be +/- or Again'd. Exceptions: new event kind itemReturn (amount, reason) in the Exceptions report (with 'Item returns by reason'), the Exceptions search, the Audit summary, and Loss Watch (at or above the void watch amount). Scan data: unchanged code; returned tobacco lines already go out with negative quantity (18) and negative final price (31), as SCAN_DATA.md allows (now documented). Tests: tests/test_item_return.cpp. Manual ch. 19 updated; Spanish strings.
- **Why:** Owner (Kevin): ITEM RETURN should prompt for the old check or a scanned item; a scanned item should show its current price book price in a window, filled in, which the cashier can change when the return is for a different amount. Owner decision 2026-11-19 15:09: sales tax is always refunded with an item return (no refund-window date check, no option to keep the tax), at the item's own tax class; with Prices include tax on, the price in the window is the tax-included price, as sales are. Defaults chosen by Bob (owner-changeable later): a manager's approval for every item return (same on-the-spot PIN pad as voids; PINs never stored or logged), a reason from a fixed list, an exception on EOD reports / Loss Watch.
- **Files:** `modern/CMakeLists.txt`, `modern/app/pos_commands.cpp`, `modern/app/pos_exceptions.cpp`, `modern/app/pos_json.cpp`, `modern/app/pos_returns.cpp`, `modern/app/pos_service.cpp`, `modern/app/pos_service.hh`, `modern/app/pos_session.cpp`, `modern/app/pos_session.hh`, `modern/app/pos_watch.cpp`, `modern/core/check.hh`, `modern/core/core.cpp`, `modern/core/report.cpp`, `modern/docs/SCAN_DATA.md`, `modern/docs/manual/manual.html`, `modern/i18n/es.json`, `modern/layout/schema.cpp`, `modern/net/remote_session.cpp`, `modern/net/remote_session.hh`, `modern/print/tickets.cpp`, `modern/seed/pages/retail-entry.json`, `modern/tests/CMakeLists.txt`, `modern/tests/test_item_return.cpp`, `modern/ui/cpp/layoutcontroller.cpp`, `modern/ui/qml/ItemReturnSheet.qml`, `modern/ui/qml/Main.qml`, `modern/ui/qml/WidgetPaymentPanel.qml`

A store whose Retail page was edited keeps its own ITEM RETURN: in edit mode set ITEM RETURN > Command > 'Item return (old sale or scan)' (BHDAN's has no action today). Stock comes back only for items whose recipe links an inventory item (as sales take it out); there is no per-item retail count. Refunds of a net-negative sale are cash only by design. ### 2026-11-17 12:49 ET — Archive helper (Claude helper) — End of Day archive: write-once PDFs beside the database, in a hash chain
- **What:** End of Day no longer writes ~/Desktop/Daily Reports (no loose pictures, no folder). The same EOD Summary PDF (reports + Exceptions + exception check pictures) is made in memory (TicketPrinter::endOfDayPdf) and kept as <data>/eod-archive/<YYYY>/<date> day <id>.pdf, mode 0444, never over a file. Table eod_archive (created IF NOT EXISTS, no schema bump, SQLite triggers refuse UPDATE/DELETE): seq, day, date, kind (archived | re-archived | moved), file, size, SHA-256, prev hash, record hash = SHA-256(prev + fields + PDF hash). A day archived again gets a re-archived record and file; the first stays. Integrity check (eod::verify) after each End of Day (worker thread), on the archive screen, and scripts/bin/vt-eod-verify (Python, same rules, read-only). Long-term archive: Store Settings > Backups > Long-term archive folder (empty by default) and days (90); older PDFs are copied there under <store>/<YYYY>/, the copy's SHA-256 checked against the chain, a NEW 'moved' record added, then the local file removed; unreachable folder (incl. an empty unmounted mount point on the same disk): nothing moves, a warning. Backups mirror eod-archive into <backups>/eod-archive and the second copy folder. Exceptions 'Tickets' button and the saveExceptionTickets page action retired (the command now says where to look). poppler-utils (pdftoppm) added to vt-install/vt-server/CI for drawing PDF pages. Tests: tests/test_eod_archive.cpp; test_print's exception test updated.
- **Why:** Owner 2026-11-17: EOD exception reports searchable from VT, not saved to the Desktop; a PDF summary of each day as the audit trail that can't be quietly changed. The chain makes a change detectable, not impossible (root on the Pi can edit files): the screens say 'any change would show'.

- **What:** Every warning, acknowledgment, manager override, cancel (and a kiosk stop sent to the bar, and a drink shared across seats) is appended as one JSON line to alcohol_policy_audit.jsonl in the store's data folder: at, time, terminal, employee, employeeId, check, checkId, seat, guest, guestKey, drinks (with this one), before, adding, item, level, action, manager, reason. ### 2026-11-16 13:17 ET — Helper C (Claude helper) — Responsible service, part 3: caution and stop at ring-in, on the Pi and the tablets
- **What:** New app/pos_over_service.cpp. PosService::serviceGate runs before a drink goes on (addItem, + on a line / setLineQuantity, Again / repeatLine, Another round): each guest's count now + this drink against the levels. Caution: serviceAsk {level caution, guest, drinks, before, adding, item, check...}; the drink waits for acknowledgeService (one tap), or cancelService. Stop: waits for overrideService(pin, reason) (a manager's or admin's PIN, with the PIN guard; a reason picked from the list or typed, kept on the check's history) or cancelService; acknowledge is refused. Then the same operation runs once more and goes through. The server decides: serviceAsk is a session property (PosSession, snapshot keys, RemoteSession Group::Check) and the commands are in pos_commands.cpp, so tablets show it and can't skip it. Self-order kiosk: no prompt; a stop says 'Please order this one at the bar'. Practice checks: never. New OverServiceOverlay.qml (in Main.qml): yellow card with Acknowledge / Not rung in; flashing red STOP with reasons, a typed reason, a PIN pad, Manager override / Not rung in. WidgetOrderList.qml: 'Seat 3: 4.2 drinks' chips (yellow at caution, red at stop) on the staff check only (not the Order summary); a 'Seats…' key on a touched drink opens 'Share across seats' (shareDrink: evenly across the seats picked; own seat again with one/none). checkInfo 'drinks' and line 'drinks'/'drinkSeats'. main.cpp sets PosShared::serviceAuditFile to <data folder>/alcohol_policy_audit.jsonl.

### 2026-11-16 11:53 ET — Helper A (Claude helper) — Prices include tax (Store Settings > Taxes)
- **What:** New store setting tax.pricesIncludeTax (default off). On: menu prices are what the guest pays; Check::totals backs each tax class's tax out of that class's part of the check total after discounts/comps (subtotal = round(part / (1 + rate)), tax = part - subtotal), never per line, so 2 x $5 at 7.35% is $10.00 = 9.32 + 0.68. Totals gains taxIncluded, itemsExTax and discountsExTax; Check::lineSales gives each line's sales without tax (running-total rounding so a class's lines add up exactly). Reports (sales summary, item/category/employee sales, accounting, royalty, food cost, menu engineering, coach, ILSA-style Z and employee sales) use the pre-tax amounts. Check bottom, payment panel, guest order, customer display and receipts show Items/Discounts, Subtotal, the tax with its rate (Alcohol tax 7.35%) and Total; the receipt says Prices include tax. The setting is frozen in a closed check's rates (JSON 'included'). Tests: tests/test_tax_included.cpp.
- **Why:** HCA (bar) prices drinks tax-included ($5, $8, $12); BHDAN (retail) keeps tax on top. Backing tax out per line rings $10.01 for 2 x $5; sales must never include tax.

### 2026-11-15 10:00 ET — Helper (Claude) for Bob — Paced updates: count the encrypted queue too
- **What:** pos_server.cpp: the old-way parts and the download stream are paced on what waits to go out including the encrypted bytes (bytesToWrite + encryptedBytesToWrite), driven by encryptedBytesWritten.

- **Files:** `modern/net/pos_server.cpp`

- **Files:** `modern/tests/test_updates.cpp`

### 2026-11-15 09:22 ET — Helper (Claude) for Bob — Tablet updates: their own resumable download; the old way paced
- **What:** Screens of this build ask for their update with fast:true and get a one-device ticket (24 h); they download the file on a second TLS connection to the store's port with the same paired-device key ({t:download, token, from} -> downloadStart + the raw bytes, streamed from disk, Range-like resume from any byte), into cache/updates/<name>.part, retrying by themselves with backoff (2 s .. 30 s) and picking up a .part after an app restart; sha256 checked before it goes to Updater.kt. Older screens (b525/b533) still get updatePart messages, but 128 KiB at a time and only once the last has mostly gone out (<256 KiB queued), so pings and answers get through. An older screen whose old-way update was cut off (or any older screen with an update, once per server start) is sent updateError on its next hello, which clears its stuck 'Getting the update... N%' and brings Update Now back. New screens clear a stuck old-way progress themselves on disconnect. The server logs a returning screen's lastDrop. Strip says when a download is waiting to go on.
- **Why:** HCA 2026-11-14/15: tablets stalled for good at 38-48% of the 160 MB APK. Cause in the code: sendUpdate read the whole file and queued all ~160 parts (~215 MB base64) on the store connection at once, so the store's pings sat behind the burst; the tablet could not answer them, the store's 15 s 'stopped answering' check (or the tablet's 10 s silent-server watchdog) cut the session part way, the queued rest was thrown away, nothing resumed, and the old client kept showing N% with Update Now hidden.
- **Files:** `modern/CMakeLists.txt`, `modern/i18n/es.json`, `modern/i18n/fr.json`, `modern/i18n/pt.json`, `modern/i18n/zh.json`, `modern/net/pos_server.cpp`, `modern/net/pos_server.hh`, `modern/net/remote_session.cpp`, `modern/net/remote_session.hh`, `modern/net/update_download.cpp`, `modern/net/update_download.hh`, `modern/tests/CMakeLists.txt`, `modern/tests/test_updates.cpp`, `modern/ui/qml/Main.qml`
- **Next person must know:** Old screens get this update the old (now paced) way once; after that updates use the fast way. Tests: modern/tests/test_updates.cpp.

### 2026-11-14 15:16 ET — Helper (Claude) for Bob — vt-touch-guard: replug a USB touchscreen missing after power-up
- **What:** New scripts/bin/vt-touch-guard (system service vt-touch-guard.service, run as root, Restart=always): learns USB touchscreens (udev ID_INPUT_TOUCHSCREEN=1 on event*, nothing model-specific) into ~/.config/viewtouch/touch-guard (port from ID_PATH, USB device, platform controller + driver from sysfs, vendor:model only to follow a moved cable). 45 s after boot then every 30 s: a remembered touchscreen missing (at once if never seen this boot; after two looks in a row if it was seen and vanished) -> one try: uhubctl per-port power cycle if uhubctl is installed and the hub supports it, else (or if that didn't bring it back in 20 s) unbind/bind of its controller in /sys/bus/platform/drivers/<driver>. At most once per 10 min and 3 per boot (counted in /run/vt-touch-guard), then one give-up line. Never resets a controller with a mounted disk on it. Logs plain words to ~/.local/state/viewtouch/touch-guard.log and the journal (logger -t vt-touch-guard). Commands: status, check, forget, sudo install, sudo uninstall. vt-server installs it once (needs-install: unit missing or older version, and no ~/.config/viewtouch/touch-guard.off from uninstall), else try-restarts it so it runs the pulled version. Manual chapter 31: new vt-touch-guard section after vt-touch-map.
- **Why:** HCA 2026-11-14: after a power-up the monitor's USB touch controller sometimes never appears (no USB device, no dmesg) until its cable is replugged.
- **Files:** `modern/docs/manual/manual.html`, `scripts/bin/vt-server`, `scripts/bin/vt-touch-guard`
- **Next person must know:** Restarting the controller reconnects everything on it (USB receipt printer /dev/usb/lp0, scanners, keyboard). Tested only against fake /sys and /proc trees (no Pi here): confirm on a Pi that ID_PATH looks like platform-xhci-hcd.N-usb-0:P and that bind brings the controller back. Paths are overridable via VT_TG_* env vars (see the script's header).

### 2026-11-13 15:25 ET — Bob (Claude) — Keyboard-mode license scans collected whole until the scanner goes quiet
- **What:** Main.qml's scan catcher: once a scan starts with @ (a license), every key is part of it until 400 ms of quiet: printable keys including the space, and line breaks as Enter or Ctrl keys; the scanner's pauses around its Ctrl keys no longer end it. ### 2026-11-13 14:54 ET — Bob (Claude) — Keyboard-mode license scans: control keys are line breaks
- **What:** Main.qml's scan catcher: during a burst that started with @ (a license), a key whose text is a control character or that comes with Ctrl (Ctrl+J, Ctrl+M, Ctrl+^) is a line break, not the end of the scan; before, any such key emptied what had been collected.
- **Why:** BHDAN's Symbol reads the license (315 characters) but the ID check still asked: its line breaks arrive as control keys, each one wiped the scan, and only the last scrap reached ViewTouch.

Now only a check with no lines is put away; anything else asks why and closes as Cancel #n. Login landing: LayoutController remembers per screen whether it was in Retail Mode (retail-entry) or hospitality (floor/menu); after a timeout or login with no terminal Start screen it lands on the Retail page, and a Quick (retail) check stays on the Retail page instead of jumping to the order screen. userPrefs has deviceStart; the Retail page is now in Terminals > Start screen's list. ### 2026-11-09 16:04 ET — Bob (Claude) — Price check and Find quicker with the 31,700-item price book
- **What:** (1) findItem (PRICE CHECK scans, typed names, addItem by name) uses a hash index of every item's id, name (case folded), number and barcodes, first item in menu order for each, made again when the menu may have changed (serial / adminRevision / size); a hit is checked against the item, and the other readings of a UPC (0 in front, either way; 11 digits without the check digit) are looked up too. (3) Find is asked 150 ms after the last key (was 200), Item Look Up 200 ms (was 300). Behaviour otherwise the same (same matches, same order).

Earlier work (2026-11-05 19:25 scans by itemForCode; 2026-11-06 17:35 Item Look Up, 17:43 Find on the main computer) left the price check scan on the old walk through every item, and Find sent 300 rows to the screen on every change.

### 2026-11-09 14:35 ET — Bob (Claude) — Serial scanners on every USB-serial port (two scanners on one register)
- **What:** SerialScanner is now one port (device + baud); new SerialScanners (ui/cpp/serialscanner.*) holds them all and is what main.cpp makes in both places (server and Linux terminal), connected to LayoutController::scanned as before. Default (SERIAL_SCANNER not set, empty or all): listen on every USB-serial port this computer has (/dev/serial/by-id/*, plus /dev/ttyUSB*, /dev/ttyACM* without a by-id name), looked for again every 5 s, so a scanner plugged into any port just works; each port reconnects on its own and logs its own 'Serial scanner on <dev> at <baud> baud'. On the server, a port a 'file' printer prints to (Store Settings > Printers; by-id link or ttyUSB name, compared by real path) is never opened, and is let go if a printer is set on it later, so a serial printer or pole display is never read as a scanner. Override: SERIAL_SCANNER=off, or a list of ports separated by commas or spaces, each @baud if wanted ('/dev/ttyUSB0, ttyUSB1@19200'; a name without / is under /dev; 'all@19200' sets the speed of the ports found). SERIAL_SCANNER_BAUD stays the default speed. No adapter- or model-specific code. Codes are handled exactly as before (a license is known by its content, core/id_card.hh, whichever port sends it). Site Setup > Scanner lists each port as 'Listening: <port> at <baud> baud' or 'Waiting for: ...' (SerialScanner::status(), siteSettings().scanners); its Auto choice is now 'Auto: every serial port (any number of scanners)' ('all' shows as Auto). vt-site: header, help and the site.conf template explain the values; vt-site show says 'Serial scanners: all: every USB-serial port'. vt-manual-shots runs with VT_SERIAL_SCANNER=off so taking manual pictures never reads the register's scanners. Manual (chapters on scanners, Site Setup, vt-site) updated; Spanish for the new phrases. Tests [scanner] (tests/test_serial_scanner.cpp): the setting read (empty, all, off, one port, lists, @baud), two scanners on pseudo-terminals (a UPC from one, a multi-line license from the other at 19200), and every-port mode (a printer's port skipped then picked up, a port set as a printer let go, an unplugged port let go, no duplicates).
- **Why:** Owner 2026-11-09: BHDAN's register gets TWO Honeywell MS1690 serial scanners on its 4-port USB-serial adapter (product scanner with Code 128 on, for cigars with only Code 128; ID scanner with Code 128 off so licenses read their PDF417). ViewTouch opened only one port. Owner 14:28: no hardware configurations coded into the system: plug a scanner into any port and it works, no setup.
- **Files:** `modern/docs/manual/manual.html`, `modern/i18n/es.json`, `modern/main.cpp`, `modern/tests/CMakeLists.txt`, `modern/tests/test_serial_scanner.cpp`, `modern/ui/cpp/serialscanner.cpp`, `modern/ui/cpp/serialscanner.hh`, `modern/ui/cpp/sitesetup.cpp`, `modern/ui/qml/WidgetSiteSetup.qml`, `scripts/bin/vt-manual-shots`, `scripts/bin/vt-site`, `scripts/bin/vt-site-lib`
- **Next person must know:** Owner's setup at BHDAN: Tripp Lite Keyspan USA-49WG; product scanner (MS1690, Code 128 on) on port 1, ID scanner (MS1690, Code 128 off) on port 2; both at 9600 baud. The Code 128 on/off is set in each scanner with Honeywell's programming barcodes, not in ViewTouch. Nothing to set in ViewTouch: after the update (vt-server) the log has one 'Serial scanner on ... at 9600 baud' line per adapter port (4 for the USA-49WG). Only if BHDAN's site.conf names one port (vt-site shows 'Serial scanners: /dev/serial/by-id/...port0') does it need clearing, on BHDAN: cd ~/viewtouch-main && vt-site set SERIAL_SCANNER "" && vt-site; beep3 -- then vt-server. Not tested on the Pi or with real scanners; built and tested only in CI.

The Terminals list says ' - its printer has no cash drawer'. Printers: Cash drawer hint 'Is a cash drawer cable plugged into this printer?' and a line listing the terminals that use it. ### 2026-11-09 10:16 ET — Bob (Claude) — USB printer: write like cat (waiting writes on their own thread)
- **What:** PrintSpooler toDevice: the device is still opened with O_NONBLOCK (a pipe with no reader fails at once) but O_NONBLOCK is then cleared, and the ticket is written with ordinary waiting writes on a short-lived thread of its own; the spooler waits up to the device time limit (10 s) for it, else gives the ticket up (kept for Reprint) while that thread waits on and closes the device when the printer takes the rest or goes away. Replaces 055736a's poll-before-close.
- **Why:** Owner 2026-11-09 on BHDAN: ViewTouch's receipt, saved to a file and sent with cat > /dev/usb/lp0, printed perfectly, but sent by ViewTouch (non-blocking writes, even with 055736a's wait) it left the TM-T88IV stuck or in error. So the receipt is fine and only the non-blocking USB writes were wrong.
- **Files:** (none)
- **Next person must know:** Not yet tried on the TM-T88IV: BHDAN is the test. If a timed-out write is still waiting, the next ticket finds the printer busy (EBUSY) until it's reset.

### 2026-11-09 09:29 ET — Bob (Claude) — USB printer: wait for the ticket to go out before closing the device
- **What:** PrintSpooler toDevice (USB /dev/usb/lp0, pipes): after the last write, poll for POLLOUT until the driver says the write has completed, then close (same 10 s limit). With O_NONBLOCK the Linux usblp driver accepts a write as soon as it is queued; closing right away cancels the unsent data (usblp_release unlinks the URBs), so receipts came out cut off or not at all.
- **Why:** Owner 2026-11-09: on BHDAN nothing printed from ViewTouch after the printer fix (554fb09), while the same printer printed and cut fine from the shell (printf > /dev/usb/lp0, a blocking write).
- **Files:** (none)
- **Next person must know:** Not tried on the TM-T88IV yet; BHDAN is the test.

### 2026-11-08 15:07 ET — Bob (Claude) — vt-copy-store: the other Pi's devices are not copied
- **What:** After copying, vt-copy-store also removes from the copy's settings the other Pi's billboards (screen billboard or a TV address), paired tablets/terminals with their keys, and serverTerm (with serverId/replicaKey as before, so its standby can't connect), and deletes printer/standby entries from the problems table; it prints each device removed. Printers and screens set up by name only (no pairing) are kept. Checked: billboard power (sleep/wake) is sent by ADB to the TV's address (pos_billboards.cpp adbArgs -> vt-add-billboard), so the ViewTouch app on the TV can't refuse it; any Pi whose adb key the TV accepts and that lists the TV can sleep it. Settings and slides come only over the billboard's own paired connection (its device key, its store's serverId), so another server can't send those.
- **Why:** Owner 2026-11-08: the copy at .225 started telling Danbury's Fire TV 'fire5' to sleep.
- **Files:** `scripts/bin/vt-copy-store`
- **Next person must know:** A TV can't tell which Pi sent an ADB sleep. To keep a second Pi off a TV, revoke that Pi's ADB key on the TV (Developer options > Revoke USB debugging authorizations, then allow only the right Pi), or have the billboard app check sleep requests itself (not built).

### 2026-11-08 15:04 ET — Bob (Claude) — USB printers: no blocking, two tries, then Reprint
- **What:** A 'file' printer that is a device (/dev/usb/lp0) or a pipe is now opened for each ticket with O_NONBLOCK, written with poll() and a 10 s limit, and closed after it (never held open). After a failure it is left alone 15 s (30, 60, 120 s after repeated failures); after 2 tries the ticket is kept for a Reprint (up to 50 per printer) and never resent on its own, and the printer is marked notPrinting: every screen shows 'Printer <name>: not printing (error at the printer). Fix it, then Reprint.' with a Reprint button (PosService::reprintHeld -> PrintSpooler::retryHeld; also from tablets), Manager > Network says 'Not printing (error at the printer): tickets wait for Reprint', and it is kept in the problems list. While it is in error a new ticket is tried once (not before the wait) and kept if it fails. A plain file printer is written as before. The spooler's timer now lets a new ticket go at once even while a device waits out its pause. Tests: test_print.cpp (a FIFO as the printer: nobody reading, then reading, then full) and test_kiosk.cpp (the alert and Reprint). Spanish strings.
- **Why:** Owner 2026-11-08: the Epson TM-T88IV on /dev/usb/lp0 went into error; ViewTouch kept the job open and retrying, and every time the printer was turned off and on (USB still plugged in) it was sent the ticket at once and tripped again; only stopping ViewTouch fixed it.
- **Files:** `modern/print/spooler.hh`, `modern/print/spooler.cpp`, `modern/main.cpp`, `modern/app/pos_service.hh`, `modern/app/pos_service.cpp`, `modern/app/pos_session.hh`, `modern/app/pos_commands.cpp`, `modern/app/pos_store_check.cpp`, `modern/ui/qml/Main.qml`, `modern/ui/qml/WidgetNetwork.qml`, `modern/tests/test_print.cpp`, `modern/tests/test_kiosk.cpp`, `modern/i18n/es.json`
- **Next person must know:** Held tickets are in memory only (gone after a restart; use Look Up Checks to reprint a receipt). Not tried on the real TM-T88IV yet.

### 2026-11-08 15:04 ET — Bob (Claude) — Standby as an option (STANDBY_ALLOWED)
- **What:** New site setting STANDBY_ALLOWED (site.conf; vt-site set STANDBY_ALLOWED yes|no; Hardware Configuration > Site Setup > This site: 'May become a standby: Yes / No'). vt-site init on a new Pi writes no; not set = yes, so existing main/standby pairs keep working. vt-server / vt-start pass it as VT_STANDBY_ALLOWED. When no and another computer already serves this store (main.cpp findMainServer), ViewTouch does not step down: it logs and shows full screen 'Another computer is already running this store at <host>. This computer is set not to become its standby (Hardware Configuration / vt-site).' (Close, or a minute) and exits with status 68 (kStandbyRefused); vt-server/vt-start print that line as 'ViewTouch didn't start: ...'. A running main that later finds a newer main of its store (its standby took over) says the same on its screens and exits 68 after 5 s instead of becoming the standby. The packaged services don't restart on 68. Spanish strings.
- **Why:** Owner 2026-11-08 14:44: failover should be an option; a Pi that is its own store must never quietly become another one's standby (the copied HCA Pi became Danbury's).
- **Files:** `scripts/bin/vt-site`, `scripts/bin/vt-site-lib`, `modern/main.cpp`, `modern/ui/cpp/sitesetup.cpp`, `modern/ui/qml/WidgetSiteSetup.qml`, `modern/packaging/vtmodern.service`, `modern/packaging/vtmodern-kiosk.service`, `modern/i18n/es.json`
- **Next person must know:** Not built: Manager > Network listing standbys with Remove/Forget. A standby connects with the store's one replicaKey (identity r:), not its own key, and only the connected one is known (PosServer::connections). Forgetting one means a new replicaKey plus dropping its connection, and a standby that can no longer connect may take over after 20 s (two mains): needs its own design. Change takes effect at the next start.

### 2026-11-08 12:58 ET — Bob (Claude) — vt-wipe-transactions: the cutover step (wipe the beta, next check 100001)
- **What:** New scripts/bin/vt-wipe-transactions (run on a store Pi): lists open checks (refuses unless --force), gift cards with a balance and customers with beta history; --dry-run shows per-table counts before/after on an in-memory copy and changes nothing. A real run asks to type WIPE, stops ViewTouch (pkill -TERM -x vtmodern), backs up the whole data folder to ~/vt-backups/before-wipe-<time>/ (each .db by SQLite online backup + quick_check; not the data folder's own backups/), then in one transaction empties checks, refunds, business_days, drawer_sessions, time_punches, parties; clears customers' visits/spending/points/last order/house account ledger, gift card activity (--keep-gift-balances yes: balance kept, beta entries become one 'carried' line; no: balance goes back, beta-only cards unsold), Changes rows for Time punches and forgotten-customer rows, promotion use counts, punch edits, checklist ticks/day, table states, event tickets sold; empties printouts/; sets meta next_check_id (--next-check, default 100001); starts vt-server unless --no-start. Deliveries, stock counts and waste are kept unless --wipe-inventory-log. ilsa-history.db is only read for the backup. PosStore::load now takes lastCheckId = max(MAX(checks.id), next_check_id - 1) and lastDayId = max(MAX(business_days.id), MAX(changes.day)). New test modern/tests/test_wipe.cpp runs the script on a made-up database.
- **Why:** Owner's transition checklist (2026-11-07): wipe every beta transaction at cutover keeping items, settings, staff, pages and the ILSA history, with a backup first; check numbering: each store's checks start at 100001 and must keep going from there with an empty checks table and after restarts. Kept price changes keep their day number, so new business days must not reuse it.
- **Files:** `modern/storage/pos_store.cpp`, `modern/tests/CMakeLists.txt`, `modern/tests/test_wipe.cpp`, `scripts/bin/vt-wipe-transactions`
- **Next person must know:** Run --dry-run first and read the gift card list: real PatronPal-imported cards are kept either way; with --keep-gift-balances no, cards sold during the beta go back to unsold. vt-server must build the new ViewTouch (this commit) before the first check after the wipe, or an old binary would number from 1. Files outside the data folder (e.g. ~/Desktop/Daily Reports PDFs, ~/.local/share/ViewTouch/*label_templates.json) are not touched.

### 2026-11-07 22:46 ET — Bob (Claude) — ILSA history: birth dates tied exactly, Z's that tie, empty Z's out, --explain-receipts, --plan for a store's copies
- **What:** Birth dates: RegSale.BDAY_ENTERED is read at import; --fill-dob ties each row to its own receipt (copy, REG_NUM, RECEIPT_NUM), takes only buyers' dates (1900 to 14 years before the sale; Access's empty 1899-12-30 and sale dates are not) and prints values, not imported from this copy, not a birth date, rows that disagree, already set, and would fill. Self-check: a tax difference of 5 cents or less is rounding (matched); a cash difference equal to change left in card/gift/check tenders, cash given back, paid outs or cash on cancelled sales is explained (matched, counted by kind); receipts that don't add up on their own are only a difference on Z's that don't tie; all explained ones are listed under 'Differences explained'. Z's with no receipts are not days (at import, and --recheck removes the ones in the file). New --explain-receipts EXPORT (read only): for receipts whose total isn't lines + tax, which column of which table (summed per receipt) or which PDetail line code equals the difference. New --plan --prefix BHNM COPY COPY COPY (read only) and --import-plan: for every day in more than one copy, receipts / sales / tenders / ILSA's Z compared, the day taken from the copy that has it whole (most receipts; a tie: the copy whose last day is newest), the days that differ printed and saved in ~/ilsa-history/plan-<store>.json; --import-plan imports each copy's days in one run (a day in from another copy is replaced). Duplicate receipts in a copy (same REG_NUM + RECEIPT_NUM) are counted and kept once; lines and tenders join their receipt's own Z.
- **Why:** Owner's dry run 2026-11-07 22:35: more receipts would get a birth date than the values counted, 7,062 of 7,192 Z's 'differed' from receipts that didn't add up, explained cash still counted as differing, the history started with an empty 2004 Z. BHNM comes as three copies with days in common.
- **Files:** `scripts/bin/vt-ilsa-history`
- **Next person must know:** Run the dry runs first and paste them; --explain-receipts says what the 72,862 receipts' extra amounts are (then the import can count it).

### 2026-11-07 16:18 ET — Bob (Claude) — vt-ilsa-history --recheck: the self-check redone from the history file, with explanations
- **What:** New vt-ilsa-history --recheck [--dry-run] [--from/--to]: rebuilds every day's totals and differences from ilsa-history.db (no export, nothing imported; a backup first unless --dry-run). The receipt check now allows the receipt discount (RT_AMT_DISCOUNTS, either sign) and a cent of rounding, and prints how every receipt was explained plus the most common unexplained differences. Tenders are compared only on Z's where ILSA saved tenders (the others are counted by year). Z_Totals columns may match a total with either sign and receipt discounts. For the Z's that still differ on tax or cash it tests what the difference equals (tax on cancelled sales, on returns, on negative receipts, on receipts dated another day; change on card/gift/check tenders, cash given back, paid outs...). A Dates section lists each copy's days that stand apart from its other days with their Z's, run times and receipt dates, and the Z's whose receipts span more than a day. 2023-02-02 Z 5828's cash is labelled as the known mistyped amount. The import uses the same check. Made-up test data gained a receipt with a receipt discount.
- **Why:** Owner's first real import 2026-11-07: the receipt check failed on almost every Z (it ignored receipt discounts), cash differed on Z's ILSA kept no tenders for, and the history started in 2004.

### 2026-11-07 14:29 ET — Bob (Claude) — Billboards: power the TV off outside its hours (adb sleep / wake)
- **What:** New billboard setting 'Power the TV off outside its hours' (Billboard defaults: on, for Fire TVs; a TV of another known make is off unless set). The store's server checks every 30 s (and at once after a change): outside its hours or Off now it runs vt-add-billboard --sleep (adb KEYCODE_SLEEP); at their start or On now --wake (KEYCODE_WAKEUP, 5 s, am start of the billboard). Not connected 60 s after waking: woken once more, then its row says "Didn't come back: check the TV's Wake on Wi-Fi setting" (On now tries again). Setting off or wake failed: the screen just goes black as before. Rows say asleep / waking / dark / on; Off now and On now buttons on its form. Each power step is logged (journal: 'Billboard power: ...') and shown in its job box. A failed sleep is tried again 10 minutes later.
- **Why:** Owner tested on fire5 (Insignia Fire TV): adb's sleep key puts the TV in standby and the wake key brings it back on the Fire home screen, so the app must be started again.

### 2026-11-06 14:26 ET — Bob (Claude) — Register window: placed on the main monitor by its exact place; no refit loop
- **What:** main.cpp present(): the register window gets setScreen(primary) and setGeometry(primary's geometry) before showFullScreen, at start and on every refit. Refit: one 800 ms timer for several screen changes at once; skipped when the window is already full screen on the primary; screen changes it causes itself (1.5 s) ignored; after 4 tries in a minute it stops and logs 'Couldn't keep the register full screen on <monitor>'.
- **Why:** Owner 2026-11-06 14:24: register on the small (guest) monitor 'giggling', desktop on the big one, though HDMI-1 (big) was primary. The refit (screenChanged -> setScreen(main) -> showNormal -> showFullScreen) restored the window's old place on the other monitor, the desktop fullscreened it there, screenChanged fired again: an endless loop.
- **Files:** `modern/main.cpp`

- **What:** Screen check shows each monitor's size and rate; 'Best mode' (its preferred size, xrandr --auto) and 'Change…' (a list of the sizes and rates it offers) per monitor, kept after reboots. ViewTouch checks the screens 20 s after start and every 10 minutes (Pi only, when vt-screens is there; not offscreen); a new problem goes on the status line once: 'Screen check: N thing(s) to fix (Manager > Hardware Configuration > Screen check)'. siteAction best:<out> and mode:<out>:<WxH>:<rate> accept only a monitor, size and rate the last look found.

### 2026-11-06 11:27 ET — Bob (Claude) — Damaged saved amounts and tax rates can't break totals
- **What:** Ported upstream 78ec1b3 (pos_json.cpp merged by hand, money.hh and its test_money.cpp test; its fuzz test left out). When a saved check is read back, amounts are held within ten billion dollars, discount and gratuity shares and tax rates to 0-100%, quantity to +/-9999, weight and cash rounding to sensible ranges, and a decimal that isn't a number becomes 0. A percent of a huge amount no longer overflows. The fork has no surcharge or tax-exempt fields, so those parts were skipped. Upstream sync ledger updated with group 2.
- **Why:** A damaged database or copied file could hold numbers that made totals overflow or come out wrong.
- **Files:** `modern/app/pos_json.cpp`, `modern/core/money.hh`, `modern/tests/test_money.cpp`, `modern/vt-modern-development-notes.md`

### 2026-11-06 11:26 ET — Bob (Claude) — Scanner: a UPC read with a 0 in front still finds the item
- **What:** Hand port of the scanner part of upstream ed80209. New PosService::sameBarcode(): a 13-digit code starting with 0 matches the 12-digit UPC on file (and the other way round), and the existing 11-digit (no check digit) match moved into it. Used by itemForCode (every scan on the register and tablets), findItem and the Item / UPC Builder search; exact matches still win first. Test added in test_customers.cpp. The upstream commit's other parts (new-item-from-unknown-code card, spreadsheet Cost column) were not taken.
- **Why:** Some scanners read a UPC-A as an EAN-13 and send a 0 in front, so the item came up as not on file.
- **Files:** `modern/app/pos_loyalty.cpp`, `modern/app/pos_menu.cpp`, `modern/app/pos_service.cpp`, `modern/app/pos_service.hh`, `modern/tests/test_customers.cpp`

- **What:** Ported upstream df89b9f (app code only; its new test file was left out). A kitchen screen can only be set to one of the store's stations (or none). Opening a table refuses an empty name, a name over 40 characters, or one with spaces at the ends. Posted messages can be at most a month ahead and twenty at a time. The command list moved into PosService::commandTable() with commandNames() for tests; invoke() works the same.
- **Why:** A screen could send any text as its station or table name (an empty or 5,000-character table showed up on the floor, receipts and reports), and posted messages had no limit, piling up in the settings every screen receives.

### 2026-11-06 11:22 ET — Bob (Claude) — Startup reads the store's tables in one go
- **What:** Ported upstream 9514708. When ViewTouch starts, it reads all of the store's tables (about thirty) inside one read transaction instead of one by one.
- **Why:** The background writer could save between two of those reads, mixing data from just before with data from just after; one read is also faster.
- **Files:** `modern/storage/pos_store.cpp`

### 2026-11-06 11:22 ET — Bob (Claude) — Forget Customer no longer freezes the screen
- **What:** Ported upstream 8d98a74. Forget Customer removes the record and today's checks at once; the earlier days' checks are searched in the background and cleaned up afterwards, then it says how many checks it cleared. Test updated to wait for that message.
- **Why:** Searching every check the store ever kept on the screen's thread held the screen for seconds on a Pi with years of sales.
- **Files:** `modern/app/pos_customers.cpp`, `modern/tests/test_safety.cpp`

### 2026-11-06 11:21 ET — Bob (Claude) — Backup restore: never leaves the store without a database
- **What:** Ported upstream 7833c83 (backup.cpp only). The data set aside before a restore always gets a name nobody else has, and if the database, its -wal or its -shm can't be moved aside, the ones already moved are put back.
- **Why:** Two restores in the same second clashed, and a half-finished move could leave the store with no database, so the next start would begin an empty one.
- **Files:** `modern/storage/backup.cpp`

### 2026-11-04 14:05 ET — Bob (Claude) — ID checks for tobacco and alcohol (license scan)
- **What:** Items in the tobacco categories ticked in Site Setup > ID checks (idCheckTobacco, tobaccoCategories, ageTobacco) and, when on, items taxed as alcohol (idCheckAlcohol, ageAlcohol) wait for the buyer's ID: AgeCheckOverlay (POS and tablets) says scan the back of the ID; core/id_card.hh reads the AAMVA PDF417 (DBB birth, DBA expiry, DAQ number; US MMDDCCYY or Canadian CCYYMMDD); under age or expired: refused (logged on the check), else the check keeps ageVerified/ageHow/ageBy/ageAt/ageId4 (last 4 only) and the item goes in; later restricted items on that check go straight in. 'Won't scan: type date' = verifyAgeHand (MMDDYYYY), logged as the exception ('ageHand' event), optionally manager PIN (idHandNeedsManager). Scans: SerialScanner keeps a license's line breaks (buffer starting '@'); keyboard-mode scanners (Bluetooth on a tablet) the same via Main.qml (Shift no longer ends a burst; idScanEnd timer). A license's small 1D barcode (Code 128/39) is ignored while an ID check waits and within 3 s of a license scan, and a license scan closes an 'Item not on file' the small one opened, so the scanner can keep reading all barcode kinds (cigar labels). Site Setup's scan test shows 'A driver's license / ID (read OK)', never the license data. Tests in test_customers.
- **Why:** Owner 2026-11-04: tablets at HCA scan the back of the ID, ID options on the Site Setup page, hand-keyed DOB the exception; the 1D barcode on licenses had forced turning that symbology off in ILSA, which broke cigar labels.
- **Files:** `modern/core/id_card.hh, modern/app/pos_age.cpp, modern/app/pos_service.cpp, modern/app/pos_service.hh, modern/app/pos_session.hh, modern/app/pos_session.cpp, modern/app/pos_commands.cpp, modern/app/pos_json.cpp, modern/core/check.hh, modern/core/settings.hh, modern/net/remote_session.hh, modern/net/remote_session.cpp, modern/ui/qml/AgeCheckOverlay.qml, modern/ui/qml/Main.qml, modern/ui/qml/WidgetSiteSetup.qml, modern/ui/cpp/layoutcontroller.cpp, modern/ui/cpp/layoutcontroller.hh, modern/ui/cpp/serialscanner.cpp, modern/CMakeLists.txt, modern/tests/test_customers.cpp`

### 2026-11-04 09:46 ET — Bob (Claude) — vt-directv scan: only this Pi's own network
- **What:** vt-directv scan no longer takes a subnet and no longer sweeps the known TVs' networks. It scans only the network of the Pi's default-route adapter (a network bigger than a /24 is swept as the Pi's /24), and SSDP answers from anywhere else are ignored. With no default route it scans nothing.
- **Why:** Owner 2026-11-04: each deployment should only look on the local subnet the Pi is on (no reaching into another network, a VPN or a second adapter's network).
- **Files:** `scripts/bin/vt-directv`
- **Next person must know:** At Hat City the receivers (192.168.6.x) will be found once the Pi is on that network; until then scan finds nothing there, which is expected.

### 2026-11-04 09:39 ET — Bob (Claude) — vt-directv rebuilt: no database writes, no frozen screen, TV scan
- **What:** scripts/bin/vt-directv rewritten. Same command line for the DIRECTV pages (box|all tune <ch> / key <key>), but it no longer rebuilds pages into viewtouch.db and seed/pages after every tune (--init-pages now just says so). TV list moved to ~/.config/viewtouch/tvs.json (built-in five bar receivers if missing). New: 'vt-directv scan [--save] [subnet]' finds DIRECTV receivers and smart TVs by SSDP plus a sweep of this computer's subnets and the known TVs' subnets (DIRECTV 8080, Roku 8060, Samsung 8001, LG webOS 3000, Android/Google 8008); 'list'. Roku TVs can be worked now (ECP keypress); Samsung/LG/Sony/Android TV are found and listed but need a one-time pairing (not built yet). 'all' sends to every receiver at once. LayoutController runs it in the background with a 10 s limit instead of waiting on the UI thread.
- **Why:** Owner's decision 2026-11-04: rebuild it, and have the Pi find DIRECTV boxes (if they're swapped) and other smart TVs to work them over the network instead of HDMI. Audit: writing viewtouch.db while ViewTouch runs breaks the one-writer rule, overwrote layout edits, rewrote repo files on the Pi (blocking git pull), and the 4 s wait froze the POS when a box was off.
- **Files:** `scripts/bin/vt-directv, modern/ui/cpp/layoutcontroller.cpp`
- **Next person must know:** Pi: sudo ln -sf ~/viewtouch-main/scripts/bin/vt-directv /usr/local/bin/vt-directv so git pull keeps it current. The Pi is on 192.168.1.x and the receivers on 192.168.6.x: SSDP won't cross that, the port sweep does if the router routes between them. The Frequently Used column on the tune pages is now fixed (edit it in the editor); counts still go to directv_freq.json.

### 2026-11-02 16:38 ET — Bob (Claude) — Tablets: wireless debugging back on after a restart
- **What:** Kiosk.ensureWirelessAdb (runs at boot): when the app holds WRITE_SECURE_SETTINGS, it sets adb_enabled, adb_wifi_enabled and adb_allowed_connection_time itself (Settings.Global). The existing DevicePolicyManager.setGlobalSetting calls for those keys always failed silently: Android doesn't let a device owner change them. Manifest declares the permission. One-time per tablet over USB: adb shell pm grant org.viewtouch.pos android.permission.WRITE_SECURE_SETTINGS (kept across app updates).
- **Why:** Owner: Bar 3 (192.168.1.210) stopped taking ADB; tcpip 5555 doesn't survive a tablet restart, so tablets drop off after every reboot.
- **Files:** `modern/android/AndroidManifest.xml`, `modern/android/src/org/viewtouch/pos/Kiosk.kt`
- **Next person must know:** Needs a new APK. With the grant + a paired Pi, wireless debugging returns after each restart and vt-adb-ensure switches it to 5555. Also reserve .220/.215/.210 for the tablets in the router (DHCP reservations) so their addresses never change.

  - Always pull before editing `modern/vt-modern-development-notes.md`, prepend new entries newest-first under the Log section using `### YYYY-MM-DD HH:MM ET — <Agent> — <short title>`, and never hand-edit `CHANGELOG.md` (generated by `tools/changelog.py`).

### 2026-11-01 16:40 ET — Bob (Claude) — Send ZPL to label printers regardless of name
- **What:** printers named "Shelf Labels"/"Stick Labels" were sent ESC/POS receipt commands and printed
  nothing. Label printers now get ZPL unless the name says receipt/Epson/kitchen; a template may force
  `"printer_language": "zpl"|"escpos"`. **Commit:** `3e86ad3`

### 2026-11-01 15:41 ET — Bob (Claude) — Claude Label Utility (bitmap label rendering)
- **What:** new label program based on `beta_label_utility.py`: every label (text and barcodes) is one
  1-bit picture at the printer's DPI, sent as ZPL `^GFA` or an image PDF — no printer fonts or printer

  printed labels. Printer resolution setting (203/300/600 dpi). Desktop launcher installer.
